# lojee-ui — Component Reference

> **Single source of truth and AI reference guide** for every component in `lojee-ui` (v0.1.0-alpha.11).
> Generated by `npm run docs:ai` from the code (TypeScript props, web-component registry, docs showcases) — **do not edit by hand**; change the component or its showcase and re-run.

## How to use this document

- Part 1 explains what applies to **every** component (install, the three ways to consume it, theming, motion, colors, sizes, accessibility, responsive behavior, customization).
- Part 2 is the **component catalog** — one section per component, in the same order as the docs site sidebar, each with the same 14 subsections: Overview, Props and Interfaces, Events, Variants, Colors and Theming, Sizes, Component States, Slots / Children / Content, Framework Interfaces, Complete Usage Examples, Component Composition, Accessibility, Responsive Behavior, Customization.
- Part 3 is an **index and quick lookups** (every web-component tag, which components emit which events).
- When asked to build UI with this library, prefer the components here over hand-rolled markup; use only props listed in the component's table; use the exact prop names (React camelCase, web-component kebab-case attributes).

# Part 1 — Global concepts

## 1.1 Installation

```bash
npm install lojee-ui
```

Peer dependencies: `lucide-react` ^1.47.0, `react` ^19.0.0, `react-dom` ^19.0.0, `tailwindcss` ^4.3.3, `maplibre-gl` >=5.0.0 (`maplibre-gl` is only needed for the Map components).

Three entry points:

| Import | What it is |
| --- | --- |
| `import { Button } from "lojee-ui"` | The React components, hooks and types. |
| `import "lojee-ui/theme.css"` | The stylesheet: design tokens (`--lojee-*` CSS variables), the light/dark themes, accent palettes, the Claymorphism design and animations. Import once at the app root. |
| `import "lojee-ui/elements"` | Registers every component as a framework-agnostic **Web Component** (`<l-button>`, `<l-modal>`, …) for Vue, Angular, Svelte, plain HTML — no React needed. |

Tailwind CSS v4 must be set up in the consuming app (the components are styled with Tailwind utility classes).

## 1.2 Three ways to consume a component

1. **React** — import the component and pass props: `<Button variant="outline" color="indigo" label="Save" />`.
2. **Web Component (Vue / Angular / plain JS / any framework)** — after `import "lojee-ui/elements"` use the `<l-*>` tag; each React prop becomes an attribute (camelCase → kebab-case), see §1.3.
3. **Data-driven shortcuts** — complex components (Sidebar, Navbar, Table, Stepper, Select, Chart, Tabs …) accept a plain-data prop (`items`, `options`, `data`, `columns`…) instead of child components, because that works identically in React and as a Web Component. Prefer the data prop when in doubt.

## 1.3 Web Component conventions (apply to every `<l-*>` tag)

- **Tag name:** `l-<kebab-case-component-name>`, e.g. `Button` → `<l-button>`, `SearchInput` → `<l-search-input>`. (HTML tag names are case-insensitive, so `<l-Button>` also works.) The exact tag of each component is listed in its *Framework Interfaces* section.
- **Attributes:** camelCase props become kebab-case attributes: `iconPosition` → `icon-position`, `defaultMode` → `default-mode`, `transitionDuration` → `transition-duration`. Setting the **property** (JS/Vue `:prop`/Angular `[prop]`) with the camelCase name always works too.
- **Types:** `string` attributes are plain text; `number` attributes are numeric strings; `boolean` attributes need an explicit value — write `disabled="true"` / `collapsed="true"` and **`show-label="false"`** (a bare attribute is read as false); `json` attributes (`items`, `options`, `data`, `columns`, `classNames`…) take a JSON string, or assign an object to the property (`el.items = [...]`; Vue `:items="items"`; Angular `[items]="items"`).
- **Children / slots:** light-DOM children are projected into the component's default slot. Components with named slots list them in *Slots / Children / Content*; use `<div slot="name">…</div>`.
- **Events:** React callback props (`onChange`) become DOM CustomEvents with the callback name minus `on`, lower-cased (`onChange` → `change`, `onCollapsedChange` → `collapsedchange`); the payload is in `event.detail`. Vue: `@change="…"`; Angular: `(change)="…"`; JS: `el.addEventListener("change", e => e.detail)`.
- **Shadow DOM:** components render into an open shadow root with the library's Tailwind styles included; `theme.css` CSS variables (inherited custom properties) cross the boundary, so theming works.

Framework templates (identical for every component — only the tag and attributes change):

```html
<!-- Plain HTML / JS -->
<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
<l-button variant="outline" color="indigo" label="Save" id="save"></l-button>
<script>document.getElementById("save").addEventListener("click", () => console.log("clicked"));</script>
```
```vue
<!-- Vue 3: tell the compiler l-* tags are custom elements (vite: vue({ template: { compilerOptions: { isCustomElement: t => t.startsWith("l-") } } })) -->
<template>
  <l-sidebar :items="items" collapsible="true" @activeitemchange="(e) => onActive(e.detail)" />
</template>
<script setup lang="ts">
import "lojee-ui/elements";
const items = [{ label: "Dashboard", icon: "home" }];
</script>
```
```ts
// Angular (standalone component)
import { Component, CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA } from "@angular/core";
import "lojee-ui/elements";

@Component({
  selector: "app-nav",
  standalone: true,
  schemas: [CUSTOM_ELEMENTS_SCHEMA],
  template: `<l-sidebar [items]="items" collapsible="true" (activeitemchange)="onActive($event.detail)" />`,
})
export class NavComponent {
  items = [{ label: "Dashboard", icon: "home" }];
  onActive(item: unknown) {}
}
```

## 1.4 Theming

Theme state is four independent settings, written as attributes on `<html>` (or on a scoped wrapper):

| Setting | Attribute | Values | Default |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mode | `data-theme` | `light`, `dark` | `light` |
| Accent | `data-accent` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, or `custom` (+ `data-accent-color="#hex"`) | `slate` |
| Design | `data-design` | `bento` (flat surfaces, thin borders, modest radii), `clay` (Claymorphism — soft, puffy, raised shapes, pressed-in fields) | `bento` |
| Active-item style | `data-active-variant` | `solid`, `outline`, `soft` — how the current item in Sidebar / Navbar / Pagination / Tabs … is drawn | `solid` |

How to set them:

- **React:** wrap the app in `<ThemeProvider defaultMode="dark" defaultAccent="orange" defaultDesign="clay" defaultActiveVariant="solid">`; read/change with the `useTheme()` hook (`{ mode, accent, design, activeVariant, setMode, setAccent, setDesign, setActiveVariant }`). Choices are persisted in `localStorage` (`lojee-ui:theme`, `lojee-ui:accent`, `lojee-ui:design`, `lojee-ui:active-variant`). `<ThemeProvider isolated>` scopes the theme to a wrapper instead of `<html>`.
- **Anything:** drop a `<ThemeSwitcher />` (`<l-theme-switcher>`) anywhere — it works with or without a provider and writes the attributes itself.
- **Without JS:** set the `data-*` attributes on `<html>` yourself.
- **Scoped:** the `App` shell and `ThemeProvider isolated` accept `theme` / `accent` / `design` / `activeVariant` to pin one region.

Design tokens (CSS custom properties, all prefixed `--lojee-`): `--lojee-surface`, `--lojee-surface-muted`, `--lojee-surface-raised`, `--lojee-fg`, `--lojee-fg-muted`, `--lojee-fg-subtle`, `--lojee-border`, `--lojee-border-strong`, and the accent palette `--lojee-accent-50 … --lojee-accent-950`. Tailwind classes use them via `bg-surface`, `bg-surface-muted`, `text-fg`, `text-fg-muted`, `text-fg-subtle`, `border-border`, `border-border-strong`, and `accent-*` (e.g. `bg-accent-600`). Override a token on any ancestor to re-skin a region.

## 1.5 Colors

Most components take a `color` prop of type `ColorName`: `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent`. `accent` follows the active theme accent (so it recolors when the user picks an accent); the others are fixed palette colors. Several components also accept any CSS color string (a hex like `"#e11d89"`) for `color` — their prop description says so.

## 1.6 Sizes

Sized components use a `size` prop — usually `sm` | `md` | `lg` (default `md`); Button / Avatar / Tooltip add `xs` and/or `xl`. Each component's *Sizes* section lists its exact values. Input-like controls share heights: sm 32px, md 40px, lg 48px.

## 1.7 Motion (shared props on most components)

| Prop | Values | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `transition` | `fade`, `slide-up`, `slide-down`, `slide-left`, `slide-right`, `zoom`, `zoom-out`, `flip`, `blur`, `bounce`, `rotate`, `drop`, `skew` | Enter transition played on mount (and for overlays on open/close). Default none. Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `transitionDuration` | number (ms) | Duration of that transition (default 450). |
| `transitionDelay` | number (ms) | Delay before it starts — handy for staggering. |
| `hoverEffect` | `lift`, `scale`, `press`, `tilt`, `ring`, `glow`, `shine` | Effect while hovering. Default none. |
| `animated` | `glow`, `pulse`, `sweep`, `bounce`, `float`, `wiggle`, `border-spin` | Looping attention animation on some components (Button, Badge, Avatar, Card, Alert, Stat, ProfileCard). Pair with `pulseColor` / `pulseGradientTo`. |

## 1.8 Icons

Icons are referenced **by name** (a string like `"mail"`, `"arrow-right"`, `"search"`) — never as components — so they survive an HTML attribute boundary. The registry is `src/core/icons.ts`; the `Icons` page of the docs site lists every available name. Props: `icon`, `leadingIcon`, `trailingIcon`, `headerIcon`, etc.

## 1.9 Accessibility conventions

- Native elements are used where possible (`<button>`, `<a>`, `<input>`); custom widgets carry the right ARIA `role` and state (`aria-expanded`, `aria-selected`, `aria-current`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-checked` …) — each component's *Accessibility* section lists what its source sets.
- Pass `aria-label` on icon-only controls (`iconOnly` buttons, search/clear buttons). Form controls accept native `id`, `name`, `aria-*`, `required`, `disabled` attributes (they spread onto the underlying element).
- Overlays (Modal, Drawer, Sheet, Popover, menus, Command Menu) close on **Escape** and on outside click; menus support arrow-key navigation.
- Focus rings use `focus-visible`; animations respect `prefers-reduced-motion`; colors meet contrast in both light and dark modes. `disabled` removes controls from the tab order.
- Label every field: use `<Label htmlFor>` (or the `label` prop where a component has one) and mark `invalid` with an error message nearby.

## 1.10 Responsive behavior

- Components are fluid (`w-full` inside their container) and mobile-first; breakpoints follow Tailwind (`sm` 640, `md` 768, `lg` 1024, `xl` 1280, `2xl` 1536). The App shell uses **container queries** (it reacts to its own width, not the viewport), collapsing `<Side>` into an off-canvas drawer below `collapseBelow`.
- Icon-only collapse: navigation components hide text labels on small screens and show tooltips.
- Each component's *Responsive Behavior* section lists the breakpoint modifiers its source uses.

## 1.11 Customization

- **`className`** — extra classes on the root element. **`classNames`** — an object of per-part class overrides (`root`, `icon`, `label`, …); each component lists its parts under *Customization*. Classes are merged with `tailwind-merge`, so a conflicting utility you pass (a different `bg-*`, `rounded-*`) **wins** over the built-in one.
- **Theme tokens** (§1.4) restyle everything at once; **`color`/accent** recolors accents; **`data-design="clay"`** switches the whole library to Claymorphism without touching components.
- **Inline `style`** is accepted by form controls; custom colors (hex) work on components that say so.

## 1.12 Composition patterns

- **Layout shell:** `<App>` with `<Top>` (Navbar), `<Side>` (Sidebar), `<Main>`, `<Footer>` — a CSS-grid shell generated from a matrix `layout` prop; `<SideToggle>` opens the drawer on small screens.
- **Forms:** `Label` + `Input`/`Textarea`/`Select`/`Checkbox`/`Radio`/`Switch` … inside a Card; submit with `Button`; ready-made `LoginForm`, `SignupForm`, `ProfileSettings`, `AccountSettings`, `PlanBilling`.
- **Overlays:** `Modal` / `Drawer` / `Sheet` / `AlertDialog` / `Popover` take an `open` flag and `onClose`; place other components inside.
- **Feedback:** `Alert`, `Toast`/`Notification`, `ProgressBar`, `Skeleton`, `LoadingState`/`EmptyState`/`ErrorState`/`SuccessState`.
- **Data display:** `Table`, `Chart`, `Stat`, `Timeline`, `ActivityFeed`, `List`, `GridView`, `DetailsList`, `Calendar`, `Map` (+ `MapMarker`, `MapRoute`), `FlowDiagram`.
- Each component's *Component Composition* section lists the other library components its own examples combine it with.

# Part 2 — Component catalog

Each section: **1 Overview · 2 Props and Interfaces · 3 Events · 4 Variants · 5 Colors and Theming · 6 Sizes · 7 Component States · 8 Slots / Children / Content · 9 Framework Interfaces · 10 Complete Usage Examples · 11 Component Composition · 12 Accessibility · 13 Responsive Behavior · 14 Customization**. Required props are marked **required**; `—` means no default.

## ── Basic Components ──

## Buttons

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Button`, `ButtonGroup`, `SegmentButton`, `SplitButton`, `SplitButtonMenuItem`.

### Buttons · 1. Component Overview

Variants, sizes, colors and states — all built with Tailwind utility classes.

- React: `import { Button } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { ButtonGroup } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { SegmentButton } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { SplitButton } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { SplitButtonMenuItem } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-button>` · `<l-button-group>` · `<l-segment-button>` · `<l-split-button>` · `<l-split-button-menu-item>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Buttons · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Button`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `pulseColor` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color — turns the pulse ring and spinning border into a gradient from `pulseColor` to this (default: solid `pulseColor`). |
| `variant` | `ButtonVariant` | `"solid"` | Visual style: "solid", "outline", "ghost", "soft", "link", "dashed", "destructive", "destructive-soft", "destructive-outline", "gradient" or "glass" (default: "solid"). |
| `color` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | `"accent"` | Button color: a built-in `ColorName` (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent) or any CSS color such as "#8b5cf6"; ignored by the destructive variants. |
| `gradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color for the gradient variant: a `ColorName` or any CSS color such as "#ec4899" (defaults to a matching preset partner). |
| `gradientDirection` | `GradientDirection` | `"to-right"` | Direction of the gradient variant: "to-right" \| "to-left" \| "to-bottom" \| "to-top" \| "to-br" \| "to-bl" \| "to-tr" \| "to-tl" (default: "to-right"). |
| `size` | `Size` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (full width) (default: "md"). |
| `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | "default" (size-based rounding), "pill" or "square" (default: "default"). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the button and dims it (default: false). |
| `loading` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a spinner in place of the icon and disables the button while true (default: false). |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "settings" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `iconOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Render as an icon-only button (no visible text) — label becomes the accessible name. |
| `iconPosition` | `"left" \| "right"` | `"left"` | Which side of the text the icon appears on: "left" or "right" (default: "left"); ignored when `iconOnly`. |
| `label` | `string` | — | Visible text (simple alternative to children) and the accessible name when iconOnly. |
| `badge` | `ReactNode` | — | Small overlay badge, e.g. a notification count. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Button content; takes precedence over `label` when both are given. |
| `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — | Called with the click event when the button is clicked (not fired while disabled or loading). |
| `type` | `"button" \| "submit" \| "reset"` | `"button"` | Native button type: "button", "submit" or "reset" (default: "button"). |
| `"aria-haspopup"` | `AriaAttributes["aria-haspopup"]` | — | For a button that toggles a disclosure (menu, listbox, etc.) it doesn't own itself. |
| `"aria-expanded"` | `boolean` | — | Whether the disclosure this button controls is currently open (controlled by the parent). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the button element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; badge?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `ButtonGroup`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The buttons (e.g. `Button` or `SegmentButton`) to join into one connected group. |
| `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | Corner treatment for the whole group (default keeps the built-in rounded-lg look). Individual segments stay square themselves — the group's outer container does the rounding, via `overflow-hidden`. |
| `onItemClick` | `(item: ButtonGroupItemClick) => void` | — | Called when one of the group's buttons is clicked, with its `index` and `label` — one handler for the whole group instead of one per button. Not called for a disabled button. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `SegmentButton`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "bold" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `active` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether this segment is currently selected/pressed — highlights it and sets `aria-pressed` (default: false); controlled by the parent. |
| `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — | Called with the click event when the segment is clicked. |
| `color` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | `"accent"` | Highlight color when active — same palette as Button (default: accent — follows the theme). Inactive segments stay neutral. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `SplitButton`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "check" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `label` **required** | `string` | — | Text of the main action button (also used to build the default menu accessible name). |
| `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — | Called with the click event when the main action button is clicked. |
| `onMenuClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — | Called with the click event when the chevron trigger is clicked, before the menu (if any) toggles. |
| `menuLabel` | `string` | — | Accessible name for the chevron trigger; defaults to `${label} options`. |
| `menuIcon` | `string` | `"chevron-down"` | Icon for the dropdown trigger — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set (default: "chevron-down"). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Dropdown content — one or more `SplitButtonMenuItem` children. When present, clicking the trigger opens a menu listing them instead of (or in addition to, if `onMenuClick` is also set) just firing a click callback. Closes on selecting an item, outside click, or Escape. |
| `variant` | `ColorVariant` | `"solid"` | Visual style shared by both halves: "solid", "outline", "ghost", "soft", "link" or "dashed" (default: "solid"). |
| `color` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | `"accent"` | Button color: a built-in `ColorName` (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent) or any CSS color such as "#8b5cf6". |
| `size` | `Size` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (default: "md"). |
| `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | Corner treatment for the whole group (default keeps the built-in rounded-lg look). The two inner buttons always render as `shape="square"` themselves — the group's outer container does the rounding, via `overflow-hidden`, so the divider between them stays a straight edge. |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables both the main button and the chevron trigger (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the button group element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; divider?: string; mainButton?: string; menuButton?: string; menu?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `SplitButtonMenuItem`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "trash-2" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The menu item's label content. |
| `onClick` | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the item is clicked; the parent menu then closes. |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the item so it can't be clicked and doesn't close the menu (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the item's button element. |

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<button>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Buttons · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — (React only) | Called with the click event when the button is clicked (not fired while disabled or loading). |
| `ButtonGroup` | `onItemClick` | `(item: ButtonGroupItemClick) => void` | — (React only) | Called when one of the group's buttons is clicked, with its `index` and `label` — one handler for the whole group instead of one per button. Not called for a disabled button. |
| `SegmentButton` | `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — (React only) | Called with the click event when the segment is clicked. |
| `SplitButton` | `onClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | — (React only) | Called with the click event when the main action button is clicked. |
| `SplitButton` | `onMenuClick` | `MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>` | `menuclick` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the click event when the chevron trigger is clicked, before the menu (if any) toggles. |
| `SplitButtonMenuItem` | `onClick` | `() => void` | — (React only) | Called with no arguments when the item is clicked; the parent menu then closes. |

### Buttons · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `variant` | `ButtonVariant` | `"solid"` | Visual style: "solid", "outline", "ghost", "soft", "link", "dashed", "destructive", "destructive-soft", "destructive-outline", "gradient" or "glass" (default: "solid"). |
| `Button` | `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | "default" (size-based rounding), "pill" or "square" (default: "default"). |
| `Button` | `type` | `button`, `submit`, `reset` | `"button"` | Native button type: "button", "submit" or "reset" (default: "button"). |
| `ButtonGroup` | `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | Corner treatment for the whole group (default keeps the built-in rounded-lg look). Individual segments stay square themselves — the group's outer container does the rounding, via `overflow-hidden`. |
| `SplitButton` | `variant` | `ColorVariant` | `"solid"` | Visual style shared by both halves: "solid", "outline", "ghost", "soft", "link" or "dashed" (default: "solid"). |
| `SplitButton` | `shape` | `Shape` | `"default"` | Corner treatment for the whole group (default keeps the built-in rounded-lg look). The two inner buttons always render as `shape="square"` themselves — the group's outer container does the rounding, via `overflow-hidden`, so the divider between them stays a straight edge. |

### Buttons · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `pulseColor` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `Button` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Button color: a built-in `ColorName` (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent) or any CSS color such as "#8b5cf6"; ignored by the destructive variants. |
| `Button` | `gradientTo` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Second color for the gradient variant: a `ColorName` or any CSS color such as "#ec4899" (defaults to a matching preset partner). |
| `SegmentButton` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Highlight color when active — same palette as Button (default: accent — follows the theme). Inactive segments stay neutral. |
| `SplitButton` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Button color: a built-in `ColorName` (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent) or any CSS color such as "#8b5cf6". |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Buttons · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `size` | `Size` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (full width) (default: "md"). |
| `SplitButton` | `size` | `Size` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (default: "md"). |

### Buttons · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `Button` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the button and dims it (default: false). |
| `Button` | `loading` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a spinner in place of the icon and disables the button while true (default: false). |
| `Button` | `iconOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Render as an icon-only button (no visible text) — label becomes the accessible name. |
| `SegmentButton` | `active` | `boolean` | `false` | Whether this segment is currently selected/pressed — highlights it and sets `aria-pressed` (default: false); controlled by the parent. |
| `SplitButton` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables both the main button and the chevron trigger (default: false). |
| `SplitButtonMenuItem` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the item so it can't be clicked and doesn't close the menu (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable) · loading (spinner, blocks clicks).

### Buttons · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Button` | `badge` | `ReactNode` | Small overlay badge, e.g. a notification count. |
| `Button` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Button content; takes precedence over `label` when both are given. |
| `ButtonGroup` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The buttons (e.g. `Button` or `SegmentButton`) to join into one connected group. |
| `SplitButton` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Dropdown content — one or more `SplitButtonMenuItem` children. When present, clicking the trigger opens a menu listing them instead of (or in addition to, if `onMenuClick` is also set) just firing a click callback. Closes on selecting an item, outside click, or Escape. |
| `SplitButtonMenuItem` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The menu item's label content. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Button groups*):

**React**
```tsx
<ButtonGroup>
  <SegmentButton icon="align-left" active={align === "left"} onClick={() => setAlign("left")} />
  <SegmentButton icon="align-center" active={align === "center"} onClick={() => setAlign("center")} />
  <SegmentButton icon="align-right" active={align === "right"} onClick={() => setAlign("right")} />
</ButtonGroup>

{/* One handler for the whole group instead of one onClick per button */}
<ButtonGroup onItemClick={({ index, label }) => console.log(index, label)}>
  <SegmentButton>Day</SegmentButton>
  <SegmentButton>Week</SegmentButton>
  <SegmentButton>Month</SegmentButton>
</ButtonGroup>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<!-- One handler on the group. event.detail is { index, label } of the clicked button;
     "this" is the group, so it can mark the clicked segment active — no ids needed. -->
<l-button-group onitemclick="[...this.children].forEach((b, i) => (b.active = i === event.detail.index))">
  <l-segment-button icon="align-left" active></l-segment-button>
  <l-segment-button icon="align-center"></l-segment-button>
  <l-segment-button icon="align-right"></l-segment-button>
</l-button-group>

<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Buttons · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Button groups

Grouped actions and a segmented toolbar control.

```tsx
<ButtonGroup>
  <SegmentButton icon="align-left" active={align === "left"} onClick={() => setAlign("left")} />
  <SegmentButton icon="align-center" active={align === "center"} onClick={() => setAlign("center")} />
  <SegmentButton icon="align-right" active={align === "right"} onClick={() => setAlign("right")} />
</ButtonGroup>

{/* One handler for the whole group instead of one onClick per button */}
<ButtonGroup onItemClick={({ index, label }) => console.log(index, label)}>
  <SegmentButton>Day</SegmentButton>
  <SegmentButton>Week</SegmentButton>
  <SegmentButton>Month</SegmentButton>
</ButtonGroup>
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. Press Replay to run the enter transitions again.

```tsx
<Button transition="fade" label="Fade" />
<Button transition="slide-up" label="Slide up" />
<Button transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} label="Slide right" />
<Button transition="zoom" label="Zoom" />
<Button transition="flip" label="Flip" />
<Button transition="blur" label="Blur" />
<Button transition="bounce" label="Bounce" />
<Button transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} label="Drop" />

<Button hoverEffect="lift" label="Lift" />
<Button hoverEffect="scale" label="Scale" />
<Button hoverEffect="press" label="Press" />
<Button hoverEffect="tilt" label="Tilt" />
<Button hoverEffect="ring" label="Ring" />
<Button hoverEffect="glow" label="Glow" />
<Button hoverEffect="shine" label="Shine" />
```

#### Animated

Attention effects via `animated`. Pulse and border-spin take a solid `pulseColor` or a gradient with `pulseGradientTo`.

```tsx
<Button animated="glow" label="Glow" />
<Button animated="pulse" label="Pulse" />
<Button animated="pulse" pulseColor="rose" pulseGradientTo="amber" label="Gradient pulse" />
<Button animated="sweep" label="Sweep" />
<Button animated="bounce" label="Bounce" />
<Button animated="float" variant="outline" label="Float" />
<Button animated="wiggle" label="Wiggle" />
<Button animated="border-spin" pulseColor="violet" pulseGradientTo="cyan" variant="soft" label="Border spin" />
```

#### Variants

Solid, outline, ghost, soft, link, dashed, and a frosted glass style.

```tsx
<Button variant="solid" label="Solid"/>
<Button variant="outline" label="Outline"/>
<Button variant="ghost" label="Ghost"/>
<Button variant="soft" label="Soft"/>
<Button variant="link" label="Link"/>
<Button variant="dashed" label="Dashed"/>
<Button variant="glass" label="Glass"/>
```

#### Sizes

From compact (xs) to prominent (xl), plus a full-width stretch.

```tsx
<Button size="xs" label="Extra small" />
<Button size="sm" label="Small" />
<Button size="md" label="Medium" />
<Button size="lg" label="Large" />
<Button size="xl" label="Extra large" />
<Button size="full" label="Full Width" />
```

_10 more examples are on the docs page._

### Buttons · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `Button`, `ButtonGroup`, `SegmentButton`, `SplitButton`, `SplitButtonMenuItem` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Buttons · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `menu`, `menuitem`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-label`, `aria-pressed`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`, `label`.

### Buttons · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Buttons · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Button`: `root`, `icon`, `badge` · `ButtonGroup`: `root` · `SegmentButton`: `root`, `icon` · `SplitButton`: `root`, `divider`, `mainButton`, `menuButton`, `menu`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Badges

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Badge`.

### Badges · 1. Component Overview

Small status/label pills — solid, outline, and soft, in every color.

- React: `import { Badge } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-badge>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Badges · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Badge`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `pulseColor` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color — turns the pulse ring and spinning border into a gradient from `pulseColor` to this (default: solid `pulseColor`). |
| `variant` | `BadgeVariant` | `"soft"` | "solid" \| "outline" \| "soft" — filled, bordered or tinted look (default: "soft"). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Badge color, one of the built-in `ColorName`s (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent). |
| `size` | `BadgeSize` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "check" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `dot` | `boolean` | `false` | Render as a small filled dot with no text — for minimal status indicators. |
| `label` | `string` | — | Visible text (simple alternative to children). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Badge content; takes precedence over `label` when both are given. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Badges · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Badges · 4. Variants

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Badge` | `variant` | `solid`, `outline`, `soft` | `"soft"` | "solid" \| "outline" \| "soft" — filled, bordered or tinted look (default: "soft"). |

| `Badge` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Badge color, one of the built-in `ColorName`s (default: "accent", which follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

### Badges · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Variants

Solid, outline, and soft.

<Badge variant="outline" label="Outline" />
<Badge variant="soft" label="Soft" />

#### Colors

Same palette as Button.

xs, sm, md, lg.

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Badges · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Badges · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Badges · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Badges · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Badge`: `root`, `icon`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Avatars

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Avatar`, `AvatarGroup`.

### Avatars · 1. Component Overview

User images with an initials fallback, status indicator, and grouping.

- React: `import { Avatar } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { AvatarGroup } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-avatar>` · `<l-avatar-group>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Avatars · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Avatar`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `pulseColor` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color — turns the pulse ring and spinning border into a gradient from `pulseColor` to this (default: solid `pulseColor`). |
| `src` | `string` | — | Image URL; falls back to `initials` when omitted or if the image fails to load. |
| `alt` | `string` | `""` | Alternative text for the image (default: ""). |
| `initials` | `string` | — | Fallback text shown when there's no image, or the image fails to load. |
| `size` | `AvatarSize` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" — 24, 32, 40, 48 or 64 px (default: "md"). |
| `shape` | `AvatarShape` | `"circle"` | "circle" or "square" (rounded corners) (default: "circle"). |
| `status` | `AvatarStatus` | — | Shows a presence dot at the bottom-right corner: "online", "offline", "busy" or "away"; omit for no dot. |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Background color for the initials fallback. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; image?: string; fallback?: string; status?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `AvatarGroup`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The `Avatar` elements to display, overlapped in a row. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Avatars · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Avatars · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Avatar` | `shape` | `circle`, `square` | `"circle"` | "circle" or "square" (rounded corners) (default: "circle"). |

### Avatars · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Avatar` | `pulseColor` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `Avatar` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Background color for the initials fallback. |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Avatars · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Avatar` | `size` | `xs`, `sm`, `md`, `lg`, `xl` | `"md"` | "xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" — 24, 32, 40, 48 or 64 px (default: "md"). |

### Avatars · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Avatar` | `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Avatars · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AvatarGroup` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The `Avatar` elements to display, overlapped in a row. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

**`Avatar`** → `<l-avatar>`

| --- | --- | --- |

| `pulseColor` | `pulse-color` | string |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `pulse-gradient-to` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Sizes*):

**React**
```tsx
<Avatar size="md" initials="AB" color="indigo" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Avatar size="md" initials="AB" color="indigo"></l-Avatar>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Avatars · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Sizes

xs, sm, md, lg, xl.

```tsx
<Avatar size="md" initials="AB" color="indigo" />
```

#### Image with fallback

Renders an <img>, falling back to initials if it fails to load.

```tsx
<Avatar src="/me.jpg" initials="LL" alt="Lojee Lim" />
```

#### Status

A small colored dot, bottom-right.

```tsx
<Avatar initials="ON" status="online" />
```

#### Shape

circle or square.

```tsx
<Avatar initials="SQ" shape="square" />
```

#### Avatar group

Overlapping stack, same idea as ButtonGroup.

```tsx
<AvatarGroup>
  <Avatar initials="AA" color="indigo" />
  <Avatar initials="BB" color="rose" />
  <Avatar initials="CC" color="emerald" />
</AvatarGroup>
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Avatars · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `Avatar`, `AvatarGroup` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Avatars · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Avatars · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`, `xl`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Avatars · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Avatar`: `root`, `image`, `fallback`, `status` · `AvatarGroup`: `root`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Icons

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Icon`.

### Icons · 1. Component Overview

A curated subset of the  Lucide icon library   — click one to copy its name. Used as a plain string via  icon="name" on any component that supports an icon prop. Need one that isn't here? Browse the full set at  lucide.dev/icons .

- React: `import { Icon } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-icon>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Icons · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Icon`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `name` **required** | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "settings" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `size` | `number` | `20` | Width and height in px (default: 20). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the icon element (e.g. a text color). |

### Icons · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Icons · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Icons · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Icons · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Icon` | `size` | `number` | `20` | Width and height in px (default: 20). |

### Icons · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Icons · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Icons · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Icon`** → `<l-icon>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `size` | `size` | number |
| `className` | `class-name` | string |

### Icons · 10. Complete Usage Examples

No packaged examples. Minimal use:

```tsx
import { Icon } from "lojee-ui";

<Icon name={…} />
```
### Icons · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Icons · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.

### Icons · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Icons · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

## Spinners

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Spinner`.

### Spinners · 1. Component Overview

Inline loading indicators — five variants, from a spinning icon to a pulsing dot.

- React: `import { Spinner } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-spinner>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Spinners · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Spinner`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `size` | `SpinnerSize` | — | Spinner size, from "xs" to "xl" (default: "md"). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | — | Spinner color, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `variant` | `SpinnerVariant` | — | Animation style: "circle", "dots", "ring", "bars" or "pulse" (default: "circle"). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `label` | `string` | — | Accessible label for screen readers (spinners carry no visible text). |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; dot?: string; bar?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Spinners · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Spinner` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Spinner color, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Spinners · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Sizes

xs through xl.

```tsx
<Spinner size="md" />
```

#### Colors

Same color palette as Button.

```tsx
<Spinner color="indigo" />
```

#### Variant

circle, dots, ring, bars, or pulse.

```tsx
<Spinner variant="circle" color="indigo" />
<Spinner variant="dots" color="indigo" />
<Spinner variant="ring" color="indigo" />
<Spinner variant="bars" color="indigo" />
<Spinner variant="pulse" color="indigo" />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`). They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<Spinner variant="circle" transition="fade" />
<Spinner variant="dots" transition="zoom" />
<Spinner variant="ring" transition="blur" />
<Spinner variant="bars" transition="bounce" />
<Spinner variant="pulse" transition="slide-up" transitionDelay={100} />
<Spinner variant="circle" transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} />
```

## Loaders

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Loader`.

### Loaders · 1. Component Overview

Skeleton placeholders for content that's still loading — distinct from Spinner's spinning/bouncing indicators.

- React: `import { Loader } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-loader>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Loaders · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Loader`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `shape` | `LoaderShape` | — | Skeleton shape: "text" (stacked lines), "circle" or "rect". Defaults to "text". |
| `variant` | `LoaderVariant` | — | Animation style for the skeleton (default: "pulse"). "wave" is like "pulse" but staggers each line's delay so multiple lines ripple instead of fading in sync — for "circle"/"rect" (a single block) it looks the same as "pulse". "none" disables animation entirely. |
| `width` | `number` | — | Pixel width, for "rect" and "circle" (defaults to a sensible size per shape). |
| `height` | `number` | — | Pixel height, for "rect" (defaults to a sensible size per shape). |
| `lines` | `number` | — | Number of stacked lines, for "text" only. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; item?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Loaders · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Loaders · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Loader` | `shape` | `text`, `circle`, `rect` | — | Skeleton shape: "text" (stacked lines), "circle" or "rect". Defaults to "text". |
| `Loader` | `variant` | `pulse`, `shimmer`, `wave`, `none` | — | Animation style for the skeleton (default: "pulse"). "wave" is like "pulse" but staggers each line's delay so multiple lines ripple instead of fading in sync — for "circle"/"rect" (a single block) it looks the same as "pulse". "none" disables animation entirely. |

### Loaders · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Loaders · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Loader` | `width` | `number` | — | Pixel width, for "rect" and "circle" (defaults to a sensible size per shape). |
| `Loader` | `height` | `number` | — | Pixel height, for "rect" (defaults to a sensible size per shape). |

### Loaders · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Loaders · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Loaders · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Loader`** → `<l-loader>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `shape` | `shape` | string |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `width` | `width` | number |
| `height` | `height` | number |
| `lines` | `lines` | number |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Text skeleton*):

**React**
```tsx
<Loader shape="text" lines={3} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Loader shape="text" lines="3"></l-Loader>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Loaders · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Text skeleton

Stacked lines, the last one shorter.

```tsx
<Loader shape="text" lines={3} />
```

#### Circle skeleton

For an avatar-shaped placeholder.

```tsx
<Loader shape="circle" width={40} />
```

#### Rect skeleton

For image/card placeholders.

```tsx
<Loader shape="rect" height={120} />
```

#### Variant

pulse (default), a sweeping shimmer highlight, or no animation at all.

```tsx
<Loader shape="rect" variant="pulse" width={140} height={80} />
<Loader shape="rect" variant="shimmer" width={140} height={80} />
<Loader shape="rect" variant="none" width={140} height={80} />
```

#### Wave variant

Like pulse, but each line's delay is staggered so they ripple instead of fading in sync.

```tsx
<Loader shape="text" variant="wave" lines={4} />
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Loaders · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.

## Dividers

*Category: Basic Components.* Exports: `Divider`.

### Dividers · 1. Component Overview

A plain rule, one with centered label text, or a draggable resize handle.

- React: `import { Divider } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-divider>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Dividers · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Divider`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `orientation` | `DividerOrientation` | `"horizontal"` | "horizontal" (default) draws a full-width line; "vertical" draws a full-height line. |
| `label` | `string` | — | Centered text (e.g. "OR") — only meaningful for horizontal, non-resizable dividers. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Custom centered content shown in place of `label` on a labeled horizontal divider. |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Line color: a built-in ColorName (default: "accent"). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `resizable` | `boolean` | `false` | Turns the divider into a draggable resize handle (mouse/touch drag, or arrow keys when focused), with a small grip pill in the middle of the line to show it can be dragged. It reports movement via `onResize` — it does NOT own any size state itself, so the consumer decides how to apply the delta (e.g. to a panel's width/height), same as a headless split-pane handle. |
| `onResize` | `(deltaPx: number) => void` | — | Called with the pointer/keyboard movement in px (positive = right/down). |
| `step` | `number` | `10` | Keyboard step size in px when resizable (default 10). |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; line?: string; label?: string; handle?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Dividers · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Divider` | `onResize` | `(deltaPx: number) => void` | `resize` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the pointer/keyboard movement in px (positive = right/down). |

### Dividers · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Divider` | `orientation` | `horizontal`, `vertical` | `"horizontal"` | "horizontal" (default) draws a full-width line; "vertical" draws a full-height line. |

### Dividers · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Divider` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Line color: a built-in ColorName (default: "accent"). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Dividers · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Dividers · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Dividers · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Divider` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Custom centered content shown in place of `label` on a labeled horizontal divider. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Dividers · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Divider`** → `<l-divider>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `orientation` | `orientation` | string |
| `label` | `label` | string |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `resizable` | `resizable` | boolean |
| `step` | `step` | number |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |

| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Plain*):

**React**
```tsx
<Divider />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Divider ></l-Divider>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Dividers · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Plain

A plain horizontal rule.

```tsx
<Divider />
```

#### With label

Centered text between two lines.

```tsx
<Divider label="OR" />
```

#### Vertical

Separating two buttons side by side.

```tsx
<Divider orientation="vertical" />
```

#### Colors

Matches the shared color palette.

```tsx
<Divider color="indigo" label="Indigo" />
```

#### Adjustable

Drag it (or focus it and use arrow keys) — it reports the movement via onResize, the consumer owns the actual size state. Try both below.

```tsx
<div className="flex">
  <div style={{ width: leftWidth }}>...</div>
  <Divider
    orientation="vertical"
    resizable
    onResize={(dx) => setLeftWidth((w) => w + dx)}
  />
  <div className="flex-1">...</div>
</div>
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Dividers · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Dividers · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `separator`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-orientation`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Dividers · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Dividers · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Divider`: `root`, `line`, `label`, `handle`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## ── Layout & Content ──

## Cards

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Card`.

### Cards · 1. Component Overview

A surface for grouping related content, with optional title and footer.

- React: `import { Card } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-card>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Cards · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Card`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `pulseColor` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color — turns the pulse ring and spinning border into a gradient from `pulseColor` to this (default: solid `pulseColor`). |
| `variant` | `CardVariant` | `"outline"` | "outline" \| "elevated" \| "soft" \| "ghost" — border, shadow, muted background or no chrome (default: "outline"). |
| `padding` | `CardPadding` | `"md"` | Inner spacing: "none" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `hoverable` | `boolean` | `false` | Adds a hover shadow (and stronger border on the outline variant) for clickable cards (default: false). |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional heading rendered above the body. |
| `footer` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional content rendered below the body, separated by a top border. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The card's body content. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; title?: string; body?: string; footer?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides (`root`, `title`, `body`, `footer`) — merged after the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Cards · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Cards · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Card` | `variant` | `outline`, `elevated`, `soft`, `ghost` | `"outline"` | "outline" \| "elevated" \| "soft" \| "ghost" — border, shadow, muted background or no chrome (default: "outline"). |

### Cards · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Card` | `pulseColor` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Cards · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Cards · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Card` | `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Cards · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Card` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Optional heading rendered above the body. |
| `Card` | `footer` | `ReactNode` | Optional content rendered below the body, separated by a top border. |
| `Card` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The card's body content. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Cards · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Card`** → `<l-card>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `padding` | `padding` | string |
| `hoverable` | `hoverable` | boolean |
| `title` | `title` | string |
| `footer` | `footer` | string |
| `animated` | `animated` | string |
| `pulseColor` | `pulse-color` | string |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `pulse-gradient-to` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Variants*):

**React**
```tsx
<Card variant="outline">Outline</Card>
<Card variant="elevated">Elevated</Card>
<Card variant="soft">Soft</Card>
<Card variant="ghost">Ghost</Card>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Card variant="outline">Outline</l-Card>
<l-Card variant="elevated">Elevated</l-Card>
<l-Card variant="soft">Soft</l-Card>
<l-Card variant="ghost">Ghost</l-Card>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Cards · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Variants

outline, elevated, soft, and ghost.

```tsx
<Card variant="outline">Outline</Card>
<Card variant="elevated">Elevated</Card>
<Card variant="soft">Soft</Card>
<Card variant="ghost">Ghost</Card>
```

#### Padding

none, sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<Card padding="sm">Small</Card>
<Card padding="lg">Large</Card>
```

#### Hoverable

Adds a shadow/border lift on hover.

```tsx
<Card hoverable>Hover me</Card>
```

#### Title and footer

A title above the body and a footer below it, separated by a border.

```tsx
<Card
  title="Plan details"
  footer={<span className="text-xs text-fg-subtle">Updated 2 days ago</span>}
>
  Your subscription renews monthly and includes unlimited seats.
</Card>
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. Press Replay to run the enter transitions again.

```tsx
<Card transition="fade">Fade</Card>
<Card transition="slide-up">Slide up</Card>
<Card transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100}>Slide right</Card>
<Card transition="zoom">Zoom</Card>
<Card transition="flip">Flip</Card>
<Card transition="blur">Blur</Card>
<Card transition="bounce">Bounce</Card>
<Card transition="drop" transitionDuration={700}>Drop</Card>

<Card hoverEffect="lift">Lift</Card>
<Card hoverEffect="glow">Glow</Card>
<Card hoverEffect="shine">Shine</Card>
<Card hoverEffect="tilt">Tilt</Card>
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Cards · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Cards · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Cards · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Cards · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Card`: `root`, `title`, `body`, `footer`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Containers

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Container`.

### Containers · 1. Component Overview

A centered, max-width wrapper for page content.

- React: `import { Container } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-container>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Containers · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Container`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `size` | `ContainerSize` | `"lg"` | Maximum width of the container: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (no max width). Defaults to "lg". |
| `centered` | `boolean` | `true` | Horizontally centers the container with auto side margins (default: true). |
| `padded` | `boolean` | `true` | Adds horizontal padding (px-4) inside the container (default: true). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Content rendered inside the container. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Containers · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Containers · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Containers · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Containers · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Container` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg`, `xl`, `full` | `"lg"` | Maximum width of the container: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (no max width). Defaults to "lg". |

### Containers · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Containers · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Container` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Content rendered inside the container. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg, xl, full — shown against a bordered wrapper so the max-width is visible.

## Sections

| `spacing` | `SectionSpacing` | `"md"` | Vertical padding of the section: "sm", "md" (default) or "lg". |

| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
