## Lists

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `List`, `ListItem`.

### Lists · 1. Component Overview

A simple ordered or unordered list, with optional dividers, borders, and item icons.

- React: `import { List } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { ListItem } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-list>` · `<l-list-item>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Lists · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `List`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ordered` | `boolean` | `false` | Renders an <ol> instead of a <ul> (default: false). |
| `variant` | `ListVariant` | `"plain"` | Row styling: "plain" (default) \| "divided" (dividers between rows) \| "bordered" (dividers plus a rounded outer border). |
| `header` | `ReactNode` | — | Content of a gray header bar above the rows (a title, a count, a button …) — no column labels. When set, the list is drawn as one rounded, bordered card with the header on top. |
| `items` | `ListItemSpec[]` | — | Rows as data — `["Overview", "Settings"]` or `[{ label: "Overview", icon: "home" }, …]` — instead of ListItem children; works the same in React and as a Web Component (`items` property / JSON attribute). Rendered before any `children`. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | List rows, typically ListItem elements. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; header?: string; list?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

#### `ListItem`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "check" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The item's label content. |
| `tooltip` | `boolean` | `false` | Wraps the row in a Tooltip showing `children` as its content and hides the visible label — handy when this item lives inside a collapsed icon-only rail (e.g. Sidebar's `collapsed` state). Keep passing `children` (the label) even when collapsed; this just controls whether it renders inline or inside the tooltip. |
| `tooltipPosition` | `TooltipPortalPosition` | `"right"` | Tooltip placement when `tooltip` is set (default: "right" — the usual fly-out direction for a left-docked collapsed rail). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; tooltip?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Lists · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Lists · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `List` | `variant` | `plain`, `divided`, `bordered` | `"plain"` | Row styling: "plain" (default) \| "divided" (dividers between rows) \| "bordered" (dividers plus a rounded outer border). |

### Lists · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Lists · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Lists · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Lists · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `List` | `header` | `ReactNode` | Content of a gray header bar above the rows (a title, a count, a button …) — no column labels. When set, the list is drawn as one rounded, bordered card with the header on top. |
| `List` | `children` | `ReactNode` | List rows, typically ListItem elements. |
| `ListItem` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The item's label content. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Lists · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`List`** → `<l-list>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `ordered` | `ordered` | boolean |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `header` | `header` | string |
| `items` | `items` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `className` | `class-name` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

**`ListItem`** → `<l-list-item>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `icon` | string |
| `tooltip` | `tooltip` | boolean |
| `tooltipPosition` | `tooltip-position` | string |
| `classNames` | `class-names` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Plain*):

**React**
```tsx
<List variant="plain" items={["Overview", "Settings", "Billing"]} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-List variant="plain" items='["Overview","Settings","Billing"]'></l-List>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Lists · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Plain

No dividers or border — just spacing.

```tsx
<List variant="plain" items={["Overview", "Settings", "Billing"]} />
```

#### Divided

A hairline divider between each item.

```tsx
<List variant="divided" items={[
  { label: "Project brief.pdf", icon: "file" },
  { label: "Cover photo.png", icon: "image" },
  { label: "Archive", icon: "folder" },
]} />
```

#### Bordered

Dividers plus an outer rounded border — reads as a self-contained card.

```tsx
<List variant="bordered" items={[
  { label: "Email verified", icon: "circle-check" },
  { label: "Password set", icon: "circle-check" },
  { label: "Two-factor auth pending", icon: "circle-alert" },
]} />
```

#### Ordered

Renders an <ol> instead of a <ul> for sequential content.

```tsx
<List ordered variant="divided" items={["Create an account", "Verify your email", "Invite your team"]} />
```

#### With tooltip

Set `tooltip` to hide the visible label and show it in a Tooltip on hover instead — handy for an icon-only rail, e.g. Sidebar's `collapsed` state.

```tsx
<List>
  <ListItem icon="home" tooltip>Dashboard</ListItem>
  <ListItem icon="folder" tooltip>Projects</ListItem>
  <ListItem icon="users" tooltip>Team</ListItem>
</List>
```

_3 more examples are on the docs page._

### Lists · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `List`, `ListItem` — use them together as shown in the examples above.
- Data-driven: pass `items` instead of child components (works the same as a Web Component).

### Lists · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `tooltip`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Lists · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Lists · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `List`: `root`, `header`, `list` · `ListItem`: `root`, `icon`, `tooltip`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Accordions

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Accordion`, `AccordionItem`.

### Accordions · 1. Component Overview

Collapsible panels built on native <details>/<summary> — zero JS state.

- React: `import { Accordion } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { AccordionItem } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-accordion>` · `<l-accordion-item>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Accordions · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Accordion`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The `AccordionItem` elements to render, stacked in a single bordered container. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the accordion's root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides (`root`) — merged after the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `AccordionItem`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Heading shown in the always-visible trigger row; clicking it expands or collapses the panel. |
| `name` | `string` | — | Shared value across sibling AccordionItems for native browser exclusive-open grouping (same mechanism as radio inputs' `name`) — the browser itself keeps only one open. Omit for an independently toggleable item. |
| `defaultOpen` | `boolean` | — | Whether the item starts expanded (uncontrolled — the browser owns the open state afterwards; default: false). |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Expands or collapses the item from outside — set it whenever you want to open / close it; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Called with the new open state whenever the item expands or collapses (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables interaction and dims the item so it cannot be toggled (default: false). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Content of the collapsible panel, shown while the item is open. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the item's root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; trigger?: string; icon?: string; panel?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides (`root`, `trigger`, `icon`, `panel`) — merged after the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Accordions · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AccordionItem` | `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | `openchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new open state whenever the item expands or collapses (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `AccordionItem` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Accordions · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Accordions · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Accordions · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Accordions · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AccordionItem` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Expands or collapses the item from outside — set it whenever you want to open / close it; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `AccordionItem` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables interaction and dims the item so it cannot be toggled (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable).

### Accordions · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Accordion` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The `AccordionItem` elements to render, stacked in a single bordered container. |
| `AccordionItem` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Heading shown in the always-visible trigger row; clicking it expands or collapses the panel. |
| `AccordionItem` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Content of the collapsible panel, shown while the item is open. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Accordions · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Accordion`** → `<l-accordion>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `className` | `class-name` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

**`AccordionItem`** → `<l-accordion-item>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `title` | `title` | string |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `defaultOpen` | `default-open` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `classNames` | `class-names` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Standalone item*):

**React**
```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem title="What is lojee-ui?" defaultOpen>
    A React + TypeScript + Tailwind component library that also ships as
    framework-agnostic Web Components.
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Accordion>
  <l-AccordionItem title="What is lojee-ui?" defaultOpen>
    A React + TypeScript + Tailwind component library that also ships as
    framework-agnostic Web Components.
  </l-AccordionItem>
</l-Accordion>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Accordions · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Standalone item

A single, independently toggleable item, open by default.

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem title="What is lojee-ui?" defaultOpen>
    A React + TypeScript + Tailwind component library that also ships as
    framework-agnostic Web Components.
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

#### Grouped (exclusive open)

Same `name` on each item — the browser keeps only one open at a time, no JS involved.

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem name="faq" title="How do I install it?" defaultOpen>
    Run `npm install lojee-ui` and import the component you need.
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem name="faq" title="Does it work outside React?">
    Yes — every component is also published as a Web Component, usable from
    any framework or plain HTML.
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem name="faq" title="Can I customize the styling?">
    Every component accepts a `className` for the root and a `classNames`
    map for its internal parts.
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

#### Independent items

Without a shared `name`, items open and close independently of each other.

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem title="Shipping">Ships within 3-5 business days.</AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem title="Returns">Free returns within 30 days of delivery.</AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

#### Disabled

A disabled item stays closed and cannot be toggled.

```tsx
<Accordion>
  <AccordionItem title="Available section">This one opens normally.</AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem title="Locked section" disabled>This content is unavailable.</AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

#### Custom styling

Override the root with className, or target the trigger, icon, or panel with classNames.

```tsx
<Accordion className="border-indigo-200 divide-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900 dark:divide-indigo-900">
  <AccordionItem
    title="Custom colors"
    defaultOpen
    classNames={{ trigger: "text-indigo-900 dark:text-indigo-200", icon: "text-indigo-400", panel: "text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300" }}
  >
    Every slot can be restyled independently via classNames.
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Accordions · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `Accordion`, `AccordionItem` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Accordions · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Accordions · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Accordions · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Accordion`: `root` · `AccordionItem`: `root`, `trigger`, `icon`, `panel`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Carousels

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Carousel`.

### Carousels · 1. Component Overview

A self-contained, data-driven slideshow with arrows and dots.

- React: `import { Carousel } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-carousel>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Carousels · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Carousel`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `slides` **required** | `ReactNode[]` | — | The slides to show, one node per slide, in order; the carousel manages the active slide itself. |
| `index` | `number` | — | The visible slide (0-indexed) — set it to move the carousel from outside; it also tracks the slide itself, so the arrows, dots and autoplay work with nothing wired up. Pair with `onChange` for two-way binding. |
| `onChange` | `(index: number) => void` | — | Called with the new slide index whenever the slide changes — the arrows, a dot, autoplay (the web component's `change` / `update` event, detail = the index). |
| `autoPlay` | `boolean` | `false` | Automatically advances to the next slide (looping) every `intervalMs` (default: false). |
| `intervalMs` | `number` | `4000` | Milliseconds between automatic slide changes when `autoPlay` is on (default: 4000). |
| `showArrows` | `boolean` | `true` | Shows previous/next arrow buttons when there is more than one slide (default: true). |
| `showDots` | `boolean` | `true` | Shows the clickable dot indicators when there is more than one slide (default: true). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; track?: string; slide?: string; arrow?: string; dot?: string; activeDot?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides (`root`, `track`, `slide`, `arrow`, `dot`, `activeDot`) — merged after the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Carousels · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Carousel` | `onChange` | `(index: number) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new slide index whenever the slide changes — the arrows, a dot, autoplay (the web component's `change` / `update` event, detail = the index). |
| `Carousel` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Carousels · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Carousels · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Carousels · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Carousels · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Carousels · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Carousel` | `slides` | `ReactNode[]` | The slides to show, one node per slide, in order; the carousel manages the active slide itself. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Carousels · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Carousel`** → `<l-carousel>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `index` | `index` | number |
| `slides` | `slides` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `autoPlay` | `auto-play` | boolean |
| `intervalMs` | `interval-ms` | number |
| `showArrows` | `show-arrows` | boolean |
| `showDots` | `show-dots` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Carousel
  slides={[
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 dark:text-indigo-300">Slide 1</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-300">Slide 2</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/50 dark:text-rose-300">Slide 3</div>,
  ]}
/>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Carousel id="basic-carousel"></l-Carousel>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  document.getElementById("basic-carousel").slides = ["Slide 1", "Slide 2", "Slide 3"];
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Carousels · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Arrows and dots, one slide at a time.

```tsx
<Carousel
  slides={[
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 dark:text-indigo-300">Slide 1</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-300">Slide 2</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/50 dark:text-rose-300">Slide 3</div>,
  ]}
/>
```

#### Autoplay

Advances automatically on an interval, still navigable via dots/arrows.

```tsx
<Carousel
  autoPlay
  intervalMs={2500}
  slides={[
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:text-amber-300">Slide 1</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-cyan-100 text-cyan-700 dark:bg-cyan-950/50 dark:text-cyan-300">Slide 2</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 dark:bg-violet-950/50 dark:text-violet-300">Slide 3</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 dark:bg-orange-950/50 dark:text-orange-300">Slide 4</div>,
  ]}
/>
```

#### Dots only

No arrows, dots only.

```tsx
<Carousel
  showArrows={false}
  slides={[
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-teal-100 text-teal-700 dark:bg-teal-950/50 dark:text-teal-300">Slide 1</div>,
    <div className="flex h-48 items-center justify-center bg-pink-100 text-pink-700 dark:bg-pink-950/50 dark:text-pink-300">Slide 2</div>,
  ]}
/>
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
const slides = [
  <div className="flex h-28 items-center justify-center bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700">Slide 1</div>,
  <div className="flex h-28 items-center justify-center bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700">Slide 2</div>,
  <div className="flex h-28 items-center justify-center bg-rose-100 text-rose-700">Slide 3</div>,
];

<Carousel slides={slides} transition="fade" />
<Carousel slides={slides} transition="slide-up" />
<Carousel slides={slides} transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} />
<Carousel slides={slides} transition="zoom" />
<Carousel slides={slides} transition="blur" />
<Carousel slides={slides} transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} />

<Carousel slides={slides} hoverEffect="lift" />
<Carousel slides={slides} hoverEffect="glow" />
<Carousel slides={slides} hoverEffect="shine" />
```

### Carousels · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Carousels · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Carousels · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Carousels · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Carousel`: `root`, `track`, `slide`, `arrow`, `dot`, `activeDot`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Images

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Image`.

### Images · 1. Component Overview

A picture with the details handled for you: it loads lazily, shows a shimmer while it loads, fades in, falls back gracefully if it can&apos;t be loaded, and sits in a frame of a fixed shape.

- React: `import { Image } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-image>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Images · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Image`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `string` | — | Image URL (or data URI). While it loads a soft shimmer is shown; if it fails, `fallback` is shown instead. |
| `alt` | `string` | `""` | Alternative text for screen readers (default: ""). Describe the picture, or leave empty for a purely decorative one. |
| `width` | `string \| number` | — | Width of the frame: any CSS length or a number of px (default: fills its container). |
| `height` | `string \| number` | — | Height of the frame: any CSS length or a number of px (default: from `ratio`, otherwise the image's own). |
| `ratio` | `ImageRatio` | `"auto"` | Fixed shape of the frame: "auto" (the image's own proportions, default), "1/1", "4/3", "3/2", "16/9" or "21/9". The image is fitted into it with `fit`. |
| `fit` | `ImageFit` | `"cover"` | How the picture fills the frame: "cover" (crop to fill, default), "contain" (show all, letterboxed), "fill" (stretch) or "none". |
| `rounded` | `ImageRadius` | `"lg"` | Corner rounding: "none" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" \| "full" (default: "lg"). |
| `loading` | `"lazy" \| "eager"` | `"lazy"` | "lazy" (default) loads the image only when it nears the viewport; "eager" loads it immediately — use eager for images above the fold. |
| `borderless` | `boolean` | `false` | Hides the border drawn around the frame (default: false). |
| `caption` | `string` | — | Short text shown under the image (default: none). |
| `fallback` | `ReactNode` | — | Content shown in place of the image when it can't be loaded or has no `src` (default: a neutral "image unavailable" placeholder). |
| `onLoad` | `() => void` | — | Called once the image has loaded. |
| `onError` | `() => void` | — | Called when the image fails to load. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; frame?: string; image?: string; caption?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Images · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Image` | `onLoad` | `() => void` | `load` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called once the image has loaded. |
| `Image` | `onError` | `() => void` | `error` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the image fails to load. |

### Images · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Images · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Images · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Image` | `width` | `string \| number` | — | Width of the frame: any CSS length or a number of px (default: fills its container). |
| `Image` | `height` | `string \| number` | — | Height of the frame: any CSS length or a number of px (default: from `ratio`, otherwise the image's own). |

### Images · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Image` | `loading` | `"lazy" \| "eager"` | `"lazy"` | "lazy" (default) loads the image only when it nears the viewport; "eager" loads it immediately — use eager for images above the fold. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · loading (spinner, blocks clicks).

### Images · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Image` | `fallback` | `ReactNode` | Content shown in place of the image when it can't be loaded or has no `src` (default: a neutral "image unavailable" placeholder). |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Images · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Image`** → `<l-image>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `src` | string |
| `alt` | `alt` | string |
| `width` | `width` | string |
| `height` | `height` | string |
| `ratio` | `ratio` | string |
| `fit` | `fit` | string |
| `rounded` | `rounded` | string |
| `loading` | `loading` | string |
| `borderless` | `borderless` | boolean |
| `caption` | `caption` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Image src="/photos/landscape.jpg" alt="A mountain lake at sunrise" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Image src="/photos/landscape.jpg" alt="A mountain lake at sunrise"></l-Image>

<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Images · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

`src` and `alt` are all you need. The frame fills its container and keeps the picture's own proportions.

```tsx
<Image src="/photos/landscape.jpg" alt="A mountain lake at sunrise" />
```

#### Ratio and fit

```tsx
<Image src={src} alt="" ratio="16/9" fit="cover" />
<Image src={src} alt="" ratio="1/1" fit="contain" />
```

#### Rounded

```tsx
<Image src={src} alt="" ratio="1/1" rounded="full" />
```

#### Fallback

If the image can't be loaded (or `src` is empty) a neutral placeholder appears; pass `fallback` to show your own content instead.

```tsx
<Image src="/missing.jpg" alt="Missing picture" ratio="4/3" />
<Image src="/missing.jpg" alt="Missing picture" ratio="4/3" fallback={<span>No preview yet</span>} />
```

#### Loading and events

```tsx
<Image
  src={src}
  alt="Hero"
  loading="eager"
  onLoad={() => console.log("loaded")}
  onError={() => console.log("failed")}
/>
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Images · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Images · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `img`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Images · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`, `xl`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Images · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Image`: `root`, `frame`, `image`, `caption`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Videos

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Video`.

### Videos · 1. Component Overview

Plays a video file or a YouTube / Vimeo link in a frame of a fixed shape, with the controls, formats, poster and error handling taken care of.

- React: `import { Video } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-video>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Videos · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Video`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `string` | — | A video file URL (mp4, webm, ogg…), or a YouTube / Vimeo page URL — those are recognised and embedded in a player automatically. |
| `sources` | `VideoSource[]` | — | Several formats of the same video; the browser plays the first one it supports. Used instead of `src` for files. |
| `poster` | `string` | — | Image shown before playback starts (files only). |
| `label` | `string` | `"Video"` | Short description of the video, used as its accessible name (default: "Video"). |
| `controls` | `boolean` | `true` | Shows the player's play / seek / volume controls (default: true). |
| `autoPlay` | `boolean` | `false` | Starts playing as soon as it can. Browsers only allow this for a muted video, so `muted` is switched on with it (default: false). |
| `muted` | `boolean` | `false` | Starts muted (default: false). |
| `loop` | `boolean` | `false` | Restarts when it reaches the end (default: false). |
| `playsInline` | `boolean` | `true` | Plays inline on phones instead of jumping to full screen (default: true). |
| `preload` | `"none" \| "metadata" \| "auto"` | `"metadata"` | How much to download up front: "none", "metadata" (default) or "auto". |
| `ratio` | `VideoRatio` | `"16/9"` | Fixed shape of the frame: "16/9" (default), "auto" (the video's own proportions), "1/1", "4/3" or "21/9". |
| `fit` | `VideoFit` | `"contain"` | How the video fills the frame: "contain" (show all, default), "cover" (crop to fill) or "fill" (stretch). Files only. |
| `rounded` | `VideoRadius` | `"lg"` | Corner rounding: "none" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl" (default: "lg"). |
| `caption` | `string` | — | Short text shown under the video (default: none). |
| `fallback` | `ReactNode` | — | Content shown when the video can't be loaded (default: a neutral "video unavailable" placeholder). |
| `onPlay` | `() => void` | — | Called when playback starts or resumes (files only). |
| `onPause` | `() => void` | — | Called when playback is paused (files only). |
| `onEnded` | `() => void` | — | Called when the video reaches the end (files only). |
| `onLoad` | `(duration: number) => void` | — | Called once the video's length is known, with its duration in seconds (files only). |
| `onError` | `() => void` | — | Called when the video can't be loaded or played (files only). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; frame?: string; video?: string; caption?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Videos · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Video` | `onPlay` | `() => void` | `play` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when playback starts or resumes (files only). |
| `Video` | `onPause` | `() => void` | `pause` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when playback is paused (files only). |
| `Video` | `onEnded` | `() => void` | `ended` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the video reaches the end (files only). |
| `Video` | `onLoad` | `(duration: number) => void` | `load` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called once the video's length is known, with its duration in seconds (files only). |
| `Video` | `onError` | `() => void` | `error` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the video can't be loaded or played (files only). |

### Videos · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Videos · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Videos · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Videos · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Videos · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Video` | `fallback` | `ReactNode` | Content shown when the video can't be loaded (default: a neutral "video unavailable" placeholder). |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Videos · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Video`** → `<l-video>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `src` | string |
| `sources` | `sources` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `poster` | `poster` | string |
| `label` | `label` | string |
| `controls` | `controls` | boolean |
| `autoPlay` | `auto-play` | boolean |
| `muted` | `muted` | boolean |
| `loop` | `loop` | boolean |
| `playsInline` | `plays-inline` | boolean |
| `preload` | `preload` | string |
| `ratio` | `ratio` | string |
| `fit` | `fit` | string |
| `rounded` | `rounded` | string |
| `caption` | `caption` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Video src="/media/intro.mp4" label="Product intro" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Video src="/media/intro.mp4" label="Product intro"></l-Video>

<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Videos · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Give it a file URL. The player controls are on by default; the frame is 16:9.

```tsx
<Video src="/media/intro.mp4" label="Product intro" />
```

#### Formats and poster

`sources` lists several formats of the same video; the browser plays the first one it supports. `poster` is the picture shown before playback starts.

```tsx
<Video
  sources={[
    { src: "/media/intro.webm", type: "video/webm" },
    { src: "/media/intro.mp4", type: "video/mp4" },
  ]}
  poster="/media/intro.jpg"
  label="Product intro"
/>
```

#### YouTube and Vimeo

A YouTube or Vimeo page URL is recognised and shown in that service's player, in the same frame. `autoPlay`, `muted` and `loop` carry over.

```tsx
<Video src="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqz-KE-bpKQ" label="Big Buck Bunny" />
<Video src="https://vimeo.com/76979871" label="The New Vimeo Player" />
```

#### Autoplay and loop

```tsx
<Video src="/media/loop.mp4" autoPlay loop controls={false} ratio="4/3" fit="cover" />
```

#### Fallback

If the video can't be loaded (or there is no `src`) a neutral placeholder appears; pass `fallback` to show your own content instead.

```tsx
<Video src="/missing.mp4" ratio="4/3" label="Missing video" />
<Video src="/missing.mp4" ratio="4/3" label="Missing video" fallback={<span>Video coming soon</span>} />
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Videos · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Videos · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `img`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Videos · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`, `xl`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Videos · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Video`: `root`, `frame`, `video`, `caption`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Code Snippet

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `CodeSnippet`, `CopyButton`.

### Code Snippet · 1. Component Overview

A code block with an optional file name and language, line numbers, syntax colouring and a copy button — plus the copy button on its own.

- React: `import { CodeSnippet } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { CopyButton } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-code-snippet>` · `<l-copy-button>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Code Snippet · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `CodeSnippet`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `code` **required** | `string` | — | The code to show. |
| `language` | `string` | — | Name of the language, shown in the header (e.g. "tsx", "bash"). Colouring covers JSX / TypeScript / HTML / CSS-style code. |
| `title` | `string` | — | Short title shown in the header, e.g. a file name. |
| `lineNumbers` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows line numbers (default: false). |
| `copyable` | `boolean` | `true` | Shows the copy button (default: true). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; header?: string; code?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

#### `CopyButton`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `text` **required** | `string` | — | The text put on the clipboard. |
| `label` | `string` | `"Copy"` | Button label (default: "Copy"). Hidden when `iconOnly` is on. |
| `copiedLabel` | `string` | `"Copied"` | Label shown for a moment after copying (default: "Copied"). |
| `iconOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows just the icon (default: false). |
| `resetAfter` | `number` | `1500` | How long the "copied" state shows, in ms (default: 1500). |
| `onCopy` | `(text: string) => void` | — | Called after the text was copied. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |

### Code Snippet · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CopyButton` | `onCopy` | `(text: string) => void` | `copy` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called after the text was copied. |

### Code Snippet · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Code Snippet · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Code Snippet · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Code Snippet · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CopyButton` | `iconOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows just the icon (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Code Snippet · 8. Slots / Children / Content

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CodeSnippet` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Code Snippet · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`CodeSnippet`** → `<l-code-snippet>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `code` | `code` | string |
| `language` | `language` | string |
| `heading` | `heading` | string |
| `lineNumbers` | `line-numbers` | boolean |
| `copyable` | `copyable` | boolean |

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<CodeSnippet code={source} title="Save.tsx" language="tsx" />
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Code Snippet · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

`code` is the text. `title` and `language` fill the header, and the copy button sits in it.

```tsx
<CodeSnippet code={source} title="Save.tsx" language="tsx" />
```

#### Line numbers and options

`lineNumbers` adds a gutter; `copyable={false}` hides the copy button; with no `title` or `language` the header disappears.

```tsx
<CodeSnippet code={source} language="tsx" lineNumbers />
<CodeSnippet code="npm install lojee-ui" copyable={false} />
```

#### Copy button

`CopyButton` works anywhere: give it the text to copy. It shows a check mark for a moment after copying, and `onCopy` reports it.

```tsx
<CopyButton text="npm install lojee-ui" />
<CopyButton text="npm install lojee-ui" iconOnly />
<CopyButton text="npm install lojee-ui" label="Copy command" copiedLabel="Copied!" />
```

### Code Snippet · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `CodeSnippet`, `CopyButton` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Code Snippet · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Code Snippet · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Code Snippet · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `CodeSnippet`: `root`, `header`, `code`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

## Iframe

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `Iframe`.

### Iframe · 1. Component Overview

Embed another page in a frame — with a loading spinner, a fixed height or aspect ratio, sandbox and permission props, and an optional address bar. Some sites refuse to be embedded and show a blank frame.

- React: `import { Iframe } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-iframe>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Iframe · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Iframe`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src` **required** | `string` | — | Address of the page to embed. |
| `title` **required** | `string` | — | Accessible name of the frame — describe what is embedded. |
| `height` | `number \| string` | `360` | Height in px or any CSS length (default: 360). Ignored when `ratio` is not "auto". |
| `ratio` | `IframeRatio` | `"auto"` | Fixed shape instead of a fixed height: "auto" (use `height`), "video" (16:9), "wide" (21:9) or "square" (default: "auto"). |
| `sandbox` | `string` | — | Restrictions applied to the embedded page, e.g. "allow-scripts allow-same-origin". Empty string applies every restriction; omit for none. |
| `allow` | `string` | — | Permissions policy of the frame, e.g. "fullscreen; clipboard-write". |
| `loading` | `"lazy" \| "eager"` | `"lazy"` | "lazy" waits until the frame is near the viewport, "eager" loads it right away (default: "lazy"). |
| `referrerPolicy` | `string` | `"strict-origin-when-cross-origin"` | Which referrer is sent with the request, e.g. "no-referrer" (default: "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"). |
| `bordered` | `boolean` | `true` | Draws a rounded border around the frame (default: true). |
| `showLoader` | `boolean` | `true` | Shows a spinner until the page has loaded (default: true). |
| `showAddress` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a small address bar with the host above the frame (default: false). |
| `onLoad` | `() => void` | — | Called when the embedded page has finished loading. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; frame?: string; address?: string; loader?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Iframe · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Iframe` | `onLoad` | `() => void` | `load` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the embedded page has finished loading. |

### Iframe · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Iframe · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Iframe · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Iframe` | `height` | `number \| string` | `360` | Height in px or any CSS length (default: 360). Ignored when `ratio` is not "auto". |

### Iframe · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Iframe` | `loading` | `"lazy" \| "eager"` | `"lazy"` | "lazy" waits until the frame is near the viewport, "eager" loads it right away (default: "lazy"). |
| `Iframe` | `bordered` | `boolean` | `true` | Draws a rounded border around the frame (default: true). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · loading (spinner, blocks clicks).

### Iframe · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Iframe · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Iframe`** → `<l-iframe>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `src` | string |
| `title` | `title` | string |
| `height` | `height` | string |
| `ratio` | `ratio` | string |
| `sandbox` | `sandbox` | string |
| `allow` | `allow` | string |
| `loading` | `loading` | string |
| `referrerPolicy` | `referrer-policy` | string |
| `bordered` | `bordered` | boolean |
| `showLoader` | `show-loader` | boolean |
| `showAddress` | `show-address` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

### Iframe · 10. Complete Usage Examples

No packaged examples. Minimal use:

```tsx
import { Iframe } from "lojee-ui";

<Iframe src={…} title={…} />
```
### Iframe · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Iframe · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Iframe · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Iframe · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Iframe`: `root`, `frame`, `address`, `loader`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Chat Box

*Category: Layout & Content.* Exports: `ChatBox`.

### Chat Box · 1. Component Overview

A chat thread with a message box — user and assistant bubbles, system notes, a "thinking" bubble and auto-scroll. Enter sends.

- React: `import { ChatBox } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-chat-box>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Chat Box · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `ChatBox`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `variant` | `ChatBoxVariant` | `"bubble"` | Look of the thread: "bubble" (filled chat bubbles), "outline" (outlined bubbles), "flat" (full-width rows, assistant rows tinted, and a plain borderless message field — like an AI assistant page) or "compact" (small text, tight spacing, no avatars) (default: "bubble"). |
| `messages` | `ChatMessage[]` | — | Messages to show (controlled) — add the user's message yourself in `onSend`. Omit to let the ChatBox keep its own list, seeded from `defaultMessages`. |
| `defaultMessages` | `ChatMessage[]` | `[]` | Starting messages when `messages` is not provided. |
| `onSend` | `(text: string) => void` | — | Called with the trimmed text when the user presses Enter or the send button. |
| `thinking` | `boolean \| string` | — | Shows the "thinking" indicator at the end of the thread. Pass the text to show — `thinking="Searching the docs"` — or `true` / an empty string for the default "Thinking" (default: off). As a web component, write `thinking="true"` for the default label, and switch it off again by setting the property to `false` (`el.thinking = false`) — removing the attribute does not clear it. |
| `thinkingVariant` | `ThinkingVariant` | `"dots"` | Style of the thinking indicator: "dots" \| "wave" \| "orb" \| "shimmer" (default: "dots"). |
| `thinkingSteps` | `string[]` | — | Status lines the thinking indicator cycles through, e.g. ["Reading the question", "Writing the answer"] — replaces the thinking text. |
| `thinkingElapsed` | `boolean` | `false` | Adds a running timer to the thinking indicator — "Thinking · 4s" (default: false). |
| `assistantIcon` | `ReactNode` | — | Icon for the assistant — in the header and beside its messages (React only; default: a sparkles icon). It is sized to fit, so pass a bare SVG / icon. |
| `suggestions` | `string[]` | — | Quick-reply chips shown above the message box until the user has sent a message; clicking one sends it like typed text. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | `"Type a message…"` | Placeholder of the message box (default: "Type a message…"). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the message box and send button (default: false). |
| `height` | `number \| string` | `440` | Height of the whole chat, in px or any CSS length such as "60vh" (default: 440). |
| `heading` | `string` | — | Title in the header bar; omit (with `subtitle`) for no header. |
| `subtitle` | `string` | — | Smaller line under the title, e.g. "Online". |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Color of your own bubbles and the send button, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `emptyText` | `string` | `"Say hello 👋"` | Text shown while there are no messages (default: "Say hello 👋"). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; header?: string; messages?: string; bubble?: string; input?: string; send?: string; suggestion?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Chat Box · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ChatBox` | `onSend` | `(text: string) => void` | `send` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the trimmed text when the user presses Enter or the send button. |

### Chat Box · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ChatBox` | `variant` | `bubble`, `outline`, `flat`, `compact` | `"bubble"` | Look of the thread: "bubble" (filled chat bubbles), "outline" (outlined bubbles), "flat" (full-width rows, assistant rows tinted, and a plain borderless message field — like an AI assistant page) or "compact" (small text, tight spacing, no avatars) (default: "bubble"). |

### Chat Box · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ChatBox` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Color of your own bubbles and the send button, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Chat Box · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ChatBox` | `height` | `number \| string` | `440` | Height of the whole chat, in px or any CSS length such as "60vh" (default: 440). |

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ChatBox` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the message box and send button (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable).

### Chat Box · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

## ── Forms & Inputs ──

## Input

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Input`.

### Input · 1. Component Overview

A text input wrapping the native <input> element.

- React: `import { Input } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-input>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Input · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Input`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `size` | `InputSize` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg". Defaults to "md". |
| `variant` | `InputVariant` | `"outline"` | Look of the field: "outline" \| "filled" \| "underline" \| "soft" \| "plain" (default: "outline"). |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Applies error (rose) styling when true (default: false). |
| `leadingIcon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "mail" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `trailingIcon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "eye" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; icon?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Input · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Input` | `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — (React only) | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `Input` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `Input` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `Input` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Input` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Input · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Input` | `variant` | `outline`, `filled`, `underline`, `soft`, `plain` | `"outline"` | Look of the field: "outline" \| "filled" \| "underline" \| "soft" \| "plain" (default: "outline"). |

### Input · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Input · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Input` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg". Defaults to "md". |

### Input · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Input` | `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Applies error (rose) styling when true (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · invalid (rose border/ring, `aria-invalid`).

### Input · 8. Slots / Children / Content

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Input` | `value` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Input` | `placeholder` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Input` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Input` | `required` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Input` | `name` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Input` | `type` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Input · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Input`** → `<l-input>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | string |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `required` | `required` | boolean |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `type` | `type` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean |
| `leadingIcon` | `leading-icon` | string |
| `trailingIcon` | `trailing-icon` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Sizes*):

**React**
```tsx
<Input size="sm" placeholder="Small" />
<Input size="md" placeholder="Medium" />
<Input size="lg" placeholder="Large" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Input size="sm" placeholder="Small"></l-Input>
<l-Input size="md" placeholder="Medium"></l-Input>
<l-Input size="lg" placeholder="Large"></l-Input>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Input · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<Input size="sm" placeholder="Small" />
<Input size="md" placeholder="Medium" />
<Input size="lg" placeholder="Large" />
```

#### Variants

outline (default), filled, underline, soft and plain (just the text, no box) — also on Textarea, PasswordInput and SearchInput.

```tsx
<Input variant="outline" placeholder="Outline" />
<Input variant="filled" placeholder="Filled" />
<Input variant="underline" placeholder="Underline" />
<Input variant="soft" placeholder="Soft" />
<Input variant="plain" placeholder="Plain" />
```

#### With icons

An optional icon on either side.

```tsx
<Input leadingIcon="mail" placeholder="Email address" />
<Input trailingIcon="eye" type="password" placeholder="Password" />
<Input leadingIcon="user" trailingIcon="circle-check" placeholder="Username" />
```

#### Invalid state

Swaps to a red border/ring, e.g. after failed validation.

```tsx
<Input invalid defaultValue="not-an-email" leadingIcon="mail" />
```

#### Invalid as a live condition

invalid is a plain boolean — pass any condition, it re-evaluates on every render.

```tsx
const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
const isInvalidEmail = email.length > 0 && !email.includes("@");

<Input
  size="md"
  invalid={isInvalidEmail}
  value={email}
  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
  placeholder="Type something…"
/>
```

_4 more examples are on the docs page._

### Input · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Input · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-invalid`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Input · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Input · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Input`: `root`, `input`, `icon`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

| `Textarea` | `required` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Textarea` | `name` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Textarea` | `rows` | `number` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Textarea · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Textarea`** → `<l-textarea>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | string |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `required` | `required` | boolean |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `rows` | `rows` | number |

| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean |

| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
