Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Textarea placeholder="Write something…" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Textarea placeholder="Write something…"></l-Textarea>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Textarea · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Plain, default resize.

```tsx
<Textarea placeholder="Write something…" />
```

#### Resize options

none, vertical (default), both.

```tsx
<Textarea resize="none" placeholder="resize: none" />
<Textarea resize="vertical" placeholder="resize: vertical" />
<Textarea resize="both" placeholder="resize: both" />
```

#### Invalid state

Swaps to a red border/ring, e.g. after failed validation.

```tsx
<Textarea invalid defaultValue="Too short" />
```

#### Disabled

Same disabled affordance as native textareas.

```tsx
<Textarea disabled placeholder="Disabled" />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<Textarea transition="fade" placeholder="Fade" />
<Textarea transition="slide-up" placeholder="Slide up" />
<Textarea transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} placeholder="Slide right" />
<Textarea transition="zoom" placeholder="Zoom" />
<Textarea transition="flip" placeholder="Flip" />
<Textarea transition="blur" placeholder="Blur" />

<Textarea hoverEffect="lift" placeholder="Lift" />
<Textarea hoverEffect="scale" placeholder="Scale" />
<Textarea hoverEffect="glow" placeholder="Glow" />
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Textarea · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Textarea · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Textarea · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Textarea · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Textarea`: `root`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Label

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Label`.

### Label · 1. Component Overview

A form field label wrapping the native <label> element.

- React: `import { Label } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-label>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Label · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Label`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `required` | `boolean` | `false` | Appends a required (*) marker after the label text (default: false). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Label text/content. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; required?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<label>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Label · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Label · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Label · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Label · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Label · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Label` | `required` | `boolean` | `false` | Appends a required (*) marker after the label text (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Label · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Label` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Label text/content. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Label` | `htmlFor` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Label · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Label`** → `<l-label>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `htmlFor` | `html-for` | string |
| `required` | `required` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Plain*):

**React**
```tsx
<Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
<Input id="email" placeholder="you@example.com" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Label htmlFor="email">Email address</l-Label>
<l-Input id="email" placeholder="you@example.com"></l-Input>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Label · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Plain

Paired with a form control via htmlFor.

```tsx
<Label htmlFor="email">Email address</Label>
<Input id="email" placeholder="you@example.com" />
```

#### Required

Adds a red asterisk after the label text.

```tsx
<Label htmlFor="name" required>Full name</Label>
<Input id="name" placeholder="Jane Doe" />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`). They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<Label transition="fade">Fade</Label>
<Label transition="slide-up">Slide up</Label>
<Label transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100}>Slide right</Label>
<Label transition="zoom">Zoom</Label>
<Label transition="flip">Flip</Label>
<Label transition="blur">Blur</Label>
<Label transition="bounce">Bounce</Label>
<Label transition="drop" transitionDuration={700}>Drop</Label>
```

### Label · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Input`.

### Label · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Label · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Label · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Label`: `root`, `required`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Checkbox

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Checkbox`.

### Checkbox · 1. Component Overview

A styled wrapper around a native checkbox input — fully keyboard/screen-reader operable.

- React: `import { Checkbox } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-checkbox>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Checkbox · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Checkbox`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `label` | `ReactNode` | — | Text or node shown beside the box; clicking it toggles the checkbox. |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Checked background color (default: accent — follows the theme). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root `<label>` element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; box?: string; label?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Checkbox · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Checkbox` | `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — (React only) | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `Checkbox` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `Checkbox` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `Checkbox` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Checkbox` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Checkbox · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Checkbox · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Checkbox` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Checked background color (default: accent — follows the theme). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Checkbox · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Checkbox · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Checkbox · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Checkbox` | `label` | `ReactNode` | Text or node shown beside the box; clicking it toggles the checkbox. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Checkbox` | `required` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Checkbox` | `checked` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Checkbox` | `defaultChecked` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Checkbox` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Checkbox` | `name` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Checkbox` | `value` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Default*):

**React**
```tsx
<Checkbox />
<Checkbox defaultChecked />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Checkbox ></l-Checkbox>
<l-Checkbox defaultChecked></l-Checkbox>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Checkbox · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Default

Unchecked and checked (defaultChecked).

```tsx
<Checkbox />
<Checkbox defaultChecked />
```

#### Colors

Same palette as Button.

```tsx
<Checkbox color="indigo" defaultChecked />
<Checkbox color="emerald" defaultChecked />
<Checkbox color="rose" defaultChecked />
```

#### Disabled

Non-interactive via the native disabled attribute.

```tsx
<Checkbox disabled />
<Checkbox disabled defaultChecked />
```

#### With label

An optional label prop rendered alongside the box.

```tsx
<Checkbox label="Accept terms and conditions" />
```

#### Events

```tsx
<Checkbox
  label="I agree to the terms"
  required
  onChange={(e) => console.log("update", e.target.checked)}
  onInput={(e) => console.log("input", e.currentTarget.checked)}
  onFocus={() => console.log("focus")}
  onInvalid={(e) => console.log("invalid", e.currentTarget.validationMessage)}
/>
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Checkbox · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Checkbox · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Checkbox · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Checkbox · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Checkbox`: `root`, `box`, `label`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Radio Group

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Radio`, `RadioGroup`.

### Radio Group · 1. Component Overview

A styled radio input, plus a RadioGroup layout wrapper — exclusivity is native, via a shared `name`.

- React: `import { Radio } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { RadioGroup } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-radio>` · `<l-radio-group>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Radio Group · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Radio`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `label` | `ReactNode` | — | Text or node rendered beside the radio; omit for a bare radio. |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Selected dot/border color (default: accent — follows the theme). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the wrapping `<label>`, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; dot?: string; label?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `RadioGroup`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `orientation` | `RadioGroupOrientation` | `"vertical"` | Stacking direction of the radios: "vertical" (default) or "horizontal" (wraps). |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The `Radio` elements to lay out; give each the same `name` so the browser makes them mutually exclusive. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

| `Radio` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `Radio` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `Radio` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Radio` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Radio Group · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Vertical group

Radios sharing a name are mutually exclusive natively — no state needed.

```tsx
<RadioGroup>
  <Radio name="plan" label="Free" defaultChecked />
  <Radio name="plan" label="Pro" />
  <Radio name="plan" label="Enterprise" />
</RadioGroup>
```

#### Horizontal group

orientation="horizontal".

```tsx
<RadioGroup orientation="horizontal">
  <Radio name="size" label="Small" defaultChecked />
  <Radio name="size" label="Medium" />
  <Radio name="size" label="Large" />
</RadioGroup>
```

#### Colors

Same palette as Button.

```tsx
<Radio name="color-indigo" color="indigo" label="Indigo" defaultChecked />
<Radio name="color-emerald" color="emerald" label="Emerald" defaultChecked />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<Radio transition="fade" label="Fade" />
<Radio transition="slide-up" label="Slide up" />
<Radio transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} label="Slide right" />
<Radio transition="zoom" label="Zoom" />
<Radio transition="flip" label="Flip" />
<Radio transition="blur" label="Blur" />
<Radio transition="bounce" label="Bounce" />
<Radio transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} label="Drop" />

<RadioGroup transition="slide-up" hoverEffect="lift">
  <Radio name="tr-plan" label="Free" defaultChecked />
  <Radio name="tr-plan" label="Pro" />
</RadioGroup>

<Radio hoverEffect="lift" label="Lift" />
<Radio hoverEffect="scale" label="Scale" />
<Radio hoverEffect="glow" label="Glow" />
<Radio hoverEffect="shine" label="Shine" />
```

#### Events

```tsx
<Radio label="Monthly" name="plan" value="monthly" required
  onChange={(e) => console.log("update", e.target.value)}
  onInput={(e) => console.log("input", e.currentTarget.value)}
  onFocus={() => console.log("focus")}
  onInvalid={(e) => console.log("invalid", e.currentTarget.validationMessage)}
/>
```

### Radio Group · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `Radio`, `RadioGroup` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Radio Group · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `radiogroup`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Radio Group · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Radio Group · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Radio`: `root`, `dot`, `label` · `RadioGroup`: `root`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Switch / Toggle

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Switch`.

### Switch / Toggle · 1. Component Overview

A styled toggle switch — a native checkbox input under the hood.

- React: `import { Switch } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-switch>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Switch / Toggle · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Switch`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `label` | `ReactNode` | — | Text or element shown next to the switch. |
| `size` | `SwitchSize` | `"md"` | Switch size: "sm", "md" or "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | "On" track color (default: accent — follows the theme). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; track?: string; thumb?: string; label?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Switch / Toggle · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Switch` | `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — (React only) | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `Switch` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `Switch` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `Switch` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Switch` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Switch / Toggle · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Switch / Toggle · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Switch` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | "On" track color (default: accent — follows the theme). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Switch / Toggle · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Switch` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | Switch size: "sm", "md" or "lg" (default: "md"). |

### Switch / Toggle · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Switch / Toggle · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Switch` | `label` | `ReactNode` | Text or element shown next to the switch. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Switch` | `required` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Switch` | `checked` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Switch` | `defaultChecked` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Switch` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Switch` | `name` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Switch / Toggle · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Switch`** → `<l-switch>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `required` | `required` | boolean |
| `checked` | `checked` | boolean |
| `defaultChecked` | `default-checked` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `label` | `label` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Default*):

**React**
```tsx
<Switch />
<Switch defaultChecked />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Switch ></l-Switch>
<l-Switch defaultChecked></l-Switch>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Switch / Toggle · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Default

Off and on (defaultChecked).

```tsx
<Switch />
<Switch defaultChecked />
```

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<Switch size="sm" defaultChecked />
<Switch size="md" defaultChecked />
<Switch size="lg" defaultChecked />
```

#### Colors

Same palette as Button.

```tsx
<Switch color="indigo" defaultChecked />
<Switch color="emerald" defaultChecked />
<Switch color="rose" defaultChecked />
```

#### Disabled

Non-interactive via the native disabled attribute.

```tsx
<Switch disabled />
<Switch disabled defaultChecked />
```

#### With label

An optional label prop rendered alongside the track.

```tsx
<Switch label="Enable notifications" />
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Switch / Toggle · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Switch / Toggle · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `switch`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Switch / Toggle · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Switch / Toggle · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Switch`: `root`, `track`, `thumb`, `label`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Select

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Select`.

### Select · 1. Component Overview

A styled native select, for accessible and robust dropdowns.

- React: `import { Select } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-select>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Select · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Select`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` **required** | `SelectOption[]` | — | The choices shown in the list (and mirrored as `<option>`s of a hidden native `<select>` for forms); each has a `label`, a `value` and an optional `disabled` flag. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | — | Hidden, disabled prompt option shown while nothing is selected; also makes the select start with no selection. |
| `size` | `SelectSize` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm", "md" (default) or "lg". |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name shown at the start of the field, e.g. "code" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set (default: none). |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Marks the field as invalid with a rose border/focus ring (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; select?: string; icon?: string; leadingIcon?: string; menu?: string; option?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<select>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Select · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Select` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |
| `Select` | `onInput` | — | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires as the user edits (typing, dragging); `detail` = the current value. React: `onInput`. |
| `Select` | `onFocus` | — | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The control gained focus; `detail` = the current value. React: `onFocus`. |
| `Select` | `onInvalid` | — | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The control failed validation (e.g. `required` and empty); `detail` = the validation message. React: `onInvalid`. |

### Select · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Select · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Select · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Select` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm", "md" (default) or "lg". |

### Select · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Select` | `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Marks the field as invalid with a rose border/focus ring (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · invalid (rose border/ring, `aria-invalid`).

### Select · 8. Slots / Children / Content

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Select` | `required` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Select` | `name` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Select` | `value` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Select` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Select · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Select`** → `<l-select>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `required` | `required` | boolean |
| `name` | `name` | string |
| `options` | `options` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `value` | `value` | string |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `icon` | `icon` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Placeholder*):

**React**
```tsx
<Select options={options} placeholder="Choose a fruit" />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Select id="fruit-select" placeholder="Choose a fruit"></l-Select>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  // options must be set via real DOM property assignment, not an attribute
  document.getElementById("fruit-select").options = [
    { label: "Apple", value: "apple" },
    { label: "Banana", value: "banana" },
    { label: "Cherry", value: "cherry", disabled: true },
    { label: "Durian", value: "durian" },
  ];
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Select · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Placeholder

A disabled, pre-selected option shown until a choice is made.

```tsx
<Select options={options} placeholder="Choose a fruit" />
```

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<Select options={options} size="sm" placeholder="Small" />
```

#### Invalid

Red border for error states.

```tsx
<Select options={options} placeholder="Choose a fruit" invalid />
```

#### Disabled option

Individual options can be disabled.

```tsx
const options = [
  { label: "Apple", value: "apple" },
  { label: "Cherry", value: "cherry", disabled: true },
];
```

#### Disabled

The whole select can be disabled.

```tsx
<Select options={options} placeholder="Choose a fruit" disabled />
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Select · 11. Component Composition

- Data-driven: pass `options` instead of child components (works the same as a Web Component).

### Select · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `combobox`, `listbox`, `option`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-controls`, `aria-describedby`, `aria-disabled`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-hidden`, `aria-invalid`, `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `aria-selected`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Select · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Select · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Select`: `root`, `select`, `icon`, `leadingIcon`, `menu`, `option`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Multi Select

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `MultiSelect`.

### Multi Select · 1. Component Overview

A button trigger showing selected items as removable chips, opening a checkbox-style dropdown listbox on click.

- React: `import { MultiSelect } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-multi-select>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Multi Select · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `MultiSelect`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` **required** | `MultiSelectOption[]` | — | The selectable options; each has a display `label`, a unique `value`, and an optional `disabled` flag. |
| `value` **required** | `string[]` | — | Controlled — array of selected `value`s. |
| `onChange` | `(value: string[]) => void` | — | Called with the new array of selected `value`s whenever an option is toggled or a chip's remove button is clicked; the consumer must store it back into `value`. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | `"Select..."` | Shown in the trigger when `value` is empty. |
| `color` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | `"accent"` | Color of the selected chips, checks, selected-option highlight and focus ring: a named color or any CSS color string such as "#7c3aed" (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; trigger?: string; tag?: string; menu?: string; option?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Multi Select · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MultiSelect` | `onChange` | `(value: string[]) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new array of selected `value`s whenever an option is toggled or a chip's remove button is clicked; the consumer must store it back into `value`. |
| `MultiSelect` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Multi Select · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Multi Select · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `MultiSelect` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Color of the selected chips, checks, selected-option highlight and focus ring: a named color or any CSS color string such as "#7c3aed" (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Multi Select · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Multi Select · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Multi Select · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Multi Select · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`MultiSelect`** → `<l-multi-select>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `options` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `value` | `value` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string[]>(["banana"]);

<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-MultiSelect id="fruit-select" placeholder="Select fruits..."></l-MultiSelect>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const options = [
    { label: "Apple", value: "apple" },
    { label: "Banana", value: "banana" },
    { label: "Cherry", value: "cherry" },
    { label: "Mango", value: "mango" },
    { label: "Papaya", value: "papaya", disabled: true },
    { label: "Watermelon", value: "watermelon" },
  ];

  const select = document.getElementById("fruit-select");
  select.options = options;
  select.value = ["banana"];
  select.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    select.value = e.detail;
  });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Multi Select · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Selected values render as removable chips inside the trigger.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string[]>(["banana"]);

<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." />
```

#### Colors

Chips, checks, the selected-option highlight and the focus ring follow `color` — a named color or any CSS color string (default: the theme accent).

```tsx
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} color="violet" />
```

#### Empty & disabled option

Empty state shows the placeholder; one option is disabled.

```tsx
<MultiSelect options={options} value={[]} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Nothing selected yet" />
```

#### Transitions

Dropdown panel transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects on the field via `hoverEffect`. Open the dropdown to see the panel animate in and out.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string[]>(["banana"]);

<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="fade" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="slide-up" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="zoom" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="flip" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." transition="bounce" transitionDuration={700} />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." hoverEffect="lift" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." hoverEffect="glow" />
<MultiSelect options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Select fruits..." hoverEffect="ring" />
```

### Multi Select · 11. Component Composition

- Data-driven: pass `options` instead of child components (works the same as a Web Component).

### Multi Select · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `button`, `listbox`, `option`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-label`, `aria-multiselectable`, `aria-selected`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Multi Select · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Multi Select · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `MultiSelect`: `root`, `trigger`, `tag`, `menu`, `option`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Combobox

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Combobox`.

### Combobox · 1. Component Overview

A single-select text input with a filtered, keyboard-navigable autocomplete dropdown.

- React: `import { Combobox } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-combobox>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Combobox · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Combobox`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` **required** | `ComboboxOption[]` | — | The selectable options; typing in the input filters them by case-insensitive label match. |
| `value` | `string` | — | Controlled selected value. |
| `onChange` | `(value: string) => void` | — | Called with the chosen option's `value` when the user selects an option by click or Enter. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | — | Placeholder text shown in the input while it is empty. |
| `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; menu?: string; option?: string; activeOption?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Combobox · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Combobox` | `onChange` | `(value: string) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the chosen option's `value` when the user selects an option by click or Enter. |
| `Combobox` | `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `Combobox` | `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `Combobox` | `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Combobox` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Combobox · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Combobox · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Combobox · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Combobox · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Combobox · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Combobox · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Combobox`** → `<l-combobox>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `options` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `value` | `value` | string |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>("manila");

<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Combobox id="city-combobox" placeholder="Search a city..."></l-Combobox>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const options = [
    { label: "Tokyo", value: "tokyo" },
    { label: "Manila", value: "manila" },
    { label: "Singapore", value: "singapore" },
    { label: "Bangkok", value: "bangkok" },
    { label: "Seoul", value: "seoul" },
    { label: "Jakarta", value: "jakarta" },
    { label: "Kuala Lumpur", value: "kuala-lumpur" },
    { label: "Hong Kong", value: "hong-kong" },
  ];

  const combobox = document.getElementById("city-combobox");
  combobox.options = options;
  combobox.value = "manila";
  combobox.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    combobox.value = e.detail;
  });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Combobox · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Type to filter, use Arrow Up/Down + Enter to select.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>("manila");

<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." />
```

#### Empty state

No value selected yet — filtering an empty query shows every option.

```tsx
<Combobox options={options} value={undefined} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." />
```

#### Transitions

Dropdown panel transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects on the field via `hoverEffect`. Open the dropdown to see the panel animate in and out.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>("manila");

<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="fade" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="slide-up" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="zoom" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="flip" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." transition="bounce" transitionDuration={700} />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." hoverEffect="lift" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." hoverEffect="glow" />
<Combobox options={options} value={value} onChange={setValue} placeholder="Search a city..." hoverEffect="ring" />
```

### Combobox · 11. Component Composition

- Data-driven: pass `options` instead of child components (works the same as a Web Component).

### Combobox · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `listbox`, `option`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-selected`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Combobox · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Combobox · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Combobox`: `root`, `input`, `menu`, `option`, `activeOption`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Date Picker

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `DatePicker`, `DateRangePicker`.

### Date Picker · 1. Component Overview

A styled native date input — the browser's own picker UI handles date selection. Plus a  DateRangePicker for start/end ranges.

- React: `import { DatePicker } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { DateRangePicker } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-date-picker>` · `<l-date-range-picker>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Date Picker · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `DatePicker`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `size` | `DatePickerSize` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg". Defaults to "md". |
| `variant` | `DatePickerVariant` | `"outline"` | Visual style: "outline" (default) \| "filled" \| "underline". |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Applies error (rose) styling when true (default: false). |
| `onClear` | `() => void` | — | Shows a clear (×) button when `value` is set — only meaningful for controlled usage. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; icon?: string; clearButton?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

#### `DateRangePicker`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `startValue` | `string` | — | yyyy-mm-dd, controlled. |
| `endValue` | `string` | — | yyyy-mm-dd, controlled. |
| `onStartChange` | `(value: string) => void` | — | Fires when the start date changes (or a preset is clicked), with the new yyyy-mm-dd string. |
| `onEndChange` | `(value: string) => void` | — | Fires when the end date changes (or a preset is clicked), with the new yyyy-mm-dd string. |
| `min` | `string` | — | Earliest selectable date (yyyy-mm-dd); the end input's minimum is the start value when set, otherwise this. |
| `max` | `string` | — | Latest selectable date (yyyy-mm-dd); the start input's maximum is the end value when set, otherwise this. |
| `size` | `DatePickerSize` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg". Defaults to "md". |
| `variant` | `DatePickerVariant` | `"outline"` | Visual style: "outline" (default) \| "filled" \| "underline". |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Applies error (rose) styling when true (default: false). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables both date inputs and all preset buttons (default: false). |
| `presets` | `DateRangePreset[]` | — | Quick-select buttons rendered below the inputs (e.g. "Last 7 days"). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; icon?: string; separator?: string; presets?: string; preset?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Date Picker · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DatePicker` | `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — (React only) | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `DatePicker` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `DatePicker` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `DatePicker` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `DatePicker` | `onClear` | `() => void` | `clear` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Shows a clear (×) button when `value` is set — only meaningful for controlled usage. |
| `DatePicker` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |
| `DateRangePicker` | `onStartChange` | `(value: string) => void` | `startchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires when the start date changes (or a preset is clicked), with the new yyyy-mm-dd string. |
| `DateRangePicker` | `onEndChange` | `(value: string) => void` | `endchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires when the end date changes (or a preset is clicked), with the new yyyy-mm-dd string. |

| `DateRangePicker` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables both date inputs and all preset buttons (default: false). |

| `DatePicker` | `value` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `DatePicker` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

### Date Picker · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

A native input type="date" with a leading icon.

```tsx
<DatePicker />
```

#### Variants

outline (default), filled, underline.

```tsx
<DatePicker variant="outline" />
<DatePicker variant="filled" />
<DatePicker variant="underline" />
```

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<DatePicker size="sm" />
```

#### With clear button

Shown when value is set and onClear is provided.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState("2026-06-15");

<DatePicker value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} onClear={() => setValue("")} />
```

#### Invalid

Red border for error states.

```tsx
<DatePicker invalid />
```

_4 more examples are on the docs page._

### Date Picker · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `DatePicker`, `DateRangePicker` — use them together as shown in the examples above.

### Date Picker · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Date Picker · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Date Picker · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `DatePicker`: `root`, `input`, `icon`, `clearButton` · `DateRangePicker`: `root`, `input`, `icon`, `separator`, `presets`, `preset`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-TimePicker ></l-TimePicker>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Time Picker · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

A native input type="time" with a leading icon.

```tsx
<TimePicker />
```

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<TimePicker size="sm" />
```

#### Invalid

Red border for error states.

```tsx
<TimePicker invalid />
```

#### Disabled

Standard native attributes like min/max/disabled pass through.

```tsx
<TimePicker disabled />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<TimePicker transition="fade" />
<TimePicker transition="slide-up" />
<TimePicker transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} />
<TimePicker transition="zoom" />
<TimePicker transition="flip" />
<TimePicker transition="blur" />
<TimePicker transition="bounce" />
<TimePicker transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} />

<TimePicker hoverEffect="lift" />
<TimePicker hoverEffect="glow" />
<TimePicker hoverEffect="ring" />
```

### Time Picker · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Time Picker · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Time Picker · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Time Picker · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `TimePicker`: `root`, `input`, `icon`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## File Upload

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `FileUpload`.

### File Upload · 1. Component Overview

A styled dropzone-style wrapper around a native, visually hidden file input.

- React: `import { FileUpload } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-file-upload>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### File Upload · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `FileUpload`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `label` | `ReactNode` | `"Click to upload or drag and drop"` | e.g. "Click to upload or drag and drop" — has a sensible default if omitted. |
| `onFilesSelected` | `(files: FileList \| null) => void` | — | Fires when the user picks files (via click or drop), with the selected FileList (or null). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; dropzone?: string; icon?: string; label?: string; fileList?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### File Upload · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FileUpload` | `onFilesSelected` | `(files: FileList \| null) => void` | `filesselected` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires when the user picks files (via click or drop), with the selected FileList (or null). |
| `FileUpload` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |
| `FileUpload` | `onInput` | — | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires as the user edits (typing, dragging); `detail` = the current value. React: `onInput`. |
| `FileUpload` | `onFocus` | — | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The control gained focus; `detail` = the current value. React: `onFocus`. |
| `FileUpload` | `onInvalid` | — | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The control failed validation (e.g. `required` and empty); `detail` = the validation message. React: `onInvalid`. |

### File Upload · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### File Upload · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### File Upload · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### File Upload · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### File Upload · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FileUpload` | `label` | `ReactNode` | e.g. "Click to upload or drag and drop" — has a sensible default if omitted. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `FileUpload` | `accept` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `FileUpload` | `multiple` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `FileUpload` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### File Upload · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`FileUpload`** → `<l-file-upload>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `label` | `label` | string |
| `accept` | `accept` | string |
| `multiple` | `multiple` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Default*):

**React**
```tsx
<FileUpload onFilesSelected={(files) => console.log(files)} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-FileUpload id="file-upload"></l-FileUpload>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  document.getElementById("file-upload")
    .addEventListener("filesselected", (e) => console.log(e.detail));
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### File Upload · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Default

Click to browse — selected file names are listed below.

```tsx
<FileUpload onFilesSelected={(files) => console.log(files)} />
```

#### Custom label & attributes

Custom label text, restricted to images, allows multiple files.

```tsx
<FileUpload
  label="Upload product photos"
  accept="image/*"
  multiple
  onFilesSelected={(files) => console.log(files)}
/>
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<FileUpload transition="fade" />
<FileUpload transition="slide-up" />
<FileUpload transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} />
<FileUpload transition="zoom" />
<FileUpload transition="flip" />
<FileUpload transition="blur" />
<FileUpload transition="bounce" />
<FileUpload transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} />

<FileUpload hoverEffect="lift" />
<FileUpload hoverEffect="glow" />
<FileUpload hoverEffect="ring" />
```

### File Upload · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### File Upload · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### File Upload · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### File Upload · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `FileUpload`: `root`, `dropzone`, `icon`, `label`, `fileList`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

<SearchInput
  value={query}
  onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
  onClear={() => setQuery("")}
  placeholder="Search…"
/>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-SearchInput id="search" placeholder="Search…"></l-SearchInput>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const search = document.getElementById("search");
  search.value = "lojee-ui";
  search.addEventListener("input", (e) => { /* e.target.value */ });
  search.addEventListener("clear", () => { search.value = ""; });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Search Input · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

The clear button only appears once there's a value and onClear is provided.

```tsx
const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

<SearchInput
  value={query}
  onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
  onClear={() => setQuery("")}
  placeholder="Search…"
/>
```

#### Sizes

sm, md, lg.

```tsx
<SearchInput size="sm" placeholder="Small" />
<SearchInput size="md" placeholder="Medium" />
<SearchInput size="lg" placeholder="Large" />
```

#### Disabled

Same disabled affordance as native inputs.

```tsx
<SearchInput disabled placeholder="Disabled" />
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<SearchInput transition="fade" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="slide-up" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="zoom" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="flip" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="blur" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="bounce" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} placeholder="Search…" />

<SearchInput hoverEffect="lift" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput hoverEffect="glow" placeholder="Search…" />
<SearchInput hoverEffect="ring" placeholder="Search…" />
```

#### Events

```tsx
<SearchInput placeholder="Search…"
  onChange={(e) => console.log("update", e.target.value)}
  onInput={(e) => console.log("input", e.currentTarget.value)}
  onFocus={() => console.log("focus")}
  onInvalid={(e) => console.log("invalid", e.currentTarget.validationMessage)}
/>
```
