
#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<PasswordInput transition="fade" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="slide-up" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="zoom" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="flip" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="blur" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="bounce" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} placeholder="Password" />

<PasswordInput hoverEffect="lift" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput hoverEffect="glow" placeholder="Password" />
<PasswordInput hoverEffect="ring" placeholder="Password" />
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Password Input · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Password Input · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-invalid`, `aria-label`, `aria-pressed`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Password Input · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Password Input · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `PasswordInput`: `root`, `input`, `toggleButton`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Slider

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Slider`.

### Slider · 1. Component Overview

A styled native range input for a single value.

- React: `import { Slider } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-slider>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Slider · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Slider`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Color of the filled part of the track (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `showValue` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows the current numeric value in a label beside the slider, kept in sync in both controlled and uncontrolled use (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; value?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

**Native attributes:** these components also accept every standard attribute of the underlying `<input>` (e.g. `id`, `name`, `value`, `defaultValue`, `placeholder`, `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly`, `min`/`max`/`step`, `aria-*`, `onChange`/`onFocus`/`onBlur`) — they are spread onto it.

### Slider · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Slider` | `onChange` | `ChangeEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | — (React only) | The value was committed — the native change event (the web component's `update` event, detail = the new value). |
| `Slider` | `onInput` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the current value). |
| `Slider` | `onFocus` | `FocusEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event). |
| `Slider` | `onInvalid` | `FormEventHandler<HTMLInputElement>` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation (e.g. `required` and empty) — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `Slider` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Slider · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Slider · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Slider` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Color of the filled part of the track (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Slider · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Slider · 7. Component States

Native states also work: `disabled`, `required`, `readOnly` (spread onto the underlying element).

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Slider · 8. Slots / Children / Content

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Slider` | `value` | `string` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Slider` | `min` | `number` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Slider` | `max` | `number` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Slider` | `step` | `number` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |
| `Slider` | `disabled` | `boolean` | Standard HTML attribute, passed straight to the underlying control. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Slider · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Slider`** → `<l-slider>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | string |
| `min` | `min` | number |
| `max` | `max` | number |
| `step` | `step` | number |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `showValue` | `show-value` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Slider defaultValue={40} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Slider value="40"></l-Slider>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Slider · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

A plain native range input.

```tsx
<Slider defaultValue={40} />
```

#### With value

Shows the current numeric value next to the track.

```tsx
<Slider defaultValue={65} showValue />
```

#### Colors

Matches the shared color palette.

```tsx
<Slider defaultValue={30} color="indigo" showValue />
```

#### Custom range

min/max/step pass through like any native range input.

```tsx
<Slider min={0} max={10} step={1} defaultValue={5} showValue />
```

#### Disabled

Standard disabled state.

```tsx
<Slider defaultValue={40} disabled />
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Slider · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Slider · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Slider · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Slider · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Slider`: `root`, `input`, `value`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Range Slider

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `RangeSlider`.

### Range Slider · 1. Component Overview

A dual-thumb range built from two overlapping native range inputs sharing one track.

- React: `import { RangeSlider } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-range-slider>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Range Slider · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `RangeSlider`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `min` | `number` | `0` | Lowest selectable value (default: 0). |
| `max` | `number` | `100` | Highest selectable value (default: 100). |
| `step` | `number` | `1` | Increment between selectable values (default: 1). |
| `value` **required** | `[number, number]` | — | Controlled `[low, high]` tuple of the two thumbs' current values. |
| `onChange` | `(value: [number, number]) => void` | — | Called with the new `[low, high]` tuple whenever either thumb is moved; the consumer must store it back into `value`. |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Color of the filled range between the thumbs (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `showValue` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows the current "low – high" text below the slider (default: false). |
| `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; track?: string; range?: string; thumb?: string; value?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Range Slider · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `RangeSlider` | `onChange` | `(value: [number, number]) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new `[low, high]` tuple whenever either thumb is moved; the consumer must store it back into `value`. |
| `RangeSlider` | `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `RangeSlider` | `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `RangeSlider` | `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `RangeSlider` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Range Slider · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Range Slider · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `RangeSlider` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Color of the filled range between the thumbs (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Range Slider · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Range Slider · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Range Slider · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Range Slider · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`RangeSlider`** → `<l-range-slider>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `min` | `min` | number |
| `max` | `max` | number |
| `step` | `step` | number |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `showValue` | `show-value` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<[number, number]>([20, 70]);

<RangeSlider value={value} onChange={setValue} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-RangeSlider id="range"></l-RangeSlider>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const range = document.getElementById("range");
  range.value = [20, 70];
  range.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    range.value = e.detail;
  });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Range Slider · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Controlled — the consumer owns the [low, high] tuple.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState<[number, number]>([20, 70]);

<RangeSlider value={value} onChange={setValue} />
```

#### With value

Shows the current [low, high] as text below the track.

```tsx
<RangeSlider value={value} onChange={setValue} color="indigo" showValue />
```

#### Custom range

Custom min/max/step — e.g. a price filter.

```tsx
<RangeSlider
  min={0}
  max={1000}
  step={10}
  value={value}
  onChange={setValue}
  color="emerald"
  showValue
/>
```

#### Transitions

Enter transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) and hover effects via `hoverEffect`. They play on mount — reload the page, or use Replay in the playground.

```tsx
<RangeSlider transition="fade" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider transition="slide-up" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider transition="slide-right" transitionDelay={100} value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider transition="zoom" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider transition="flip" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider transition="blur" value={[20, 70]} />

<RangeSlider hoverEffect="lift" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider hoverEffect="scale" value={[20, 70]} />
<RangeSlider hoverEffect="glow" value={[20, 70]} />
```

### Range Slider · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Range Slider · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Range Slider · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Range Slider · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `RangeSlider`: `root`, `track`, `range`, `thumb`, `value`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Tag Input

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `TagInput`.

### Tag Input · 1. Component Overview

A text field that turns what you type into removable tags. Enter or a comma adds one; Backspace on an empty field removes the last.

- React: `import { TagInput } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-tag-input>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Tag Input · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `TagInput`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `string[]` | — | The current tags. Also settable from outside; the input keeps its own list otherwise, so it works with nothing wired up. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | `"Add a tag…"` | Placeholder shown while there are no tags and nothing is typed (default: "Add a tag…"). |
| `maxTags` | `number` | — | Most tags allowed; once reached, typing is disabled (default: no limit). |
| `allowDuplicates` | `boolean` | `false` | Allow the same tag twice (default: false — a repeat is ignored). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Tag color: a built-in ColorName (default: "accent"). |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red border for error states (default: false). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the input and the remove buttons (default: false). |
| `onChange` | `(tags: string[]) => void` | — | Called with the full list of tags whenever a tag is added or removed. |
| `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; tag?: string; input?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

### Tag Input · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TagInput` | `onChange` | `(tags: string[]) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the full list of tags whenever a tag is added or removed. |
| `TagInput` | `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `TagInput` | `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `TagInput` | `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `TagInput` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Tag Input · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Tag Input · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TagInput` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Tag color: a built-in ColorName (default: "accent"). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Tag Input · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Tag Input · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TagInput` | `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red border for error states (default: false). |
| `TagInput` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the input and the remove buttons (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable) · invalid (rose border/ring, `aria-invalid`).

### Tag Input · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Tag Input · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`TagInput`** → `<l-tag-input>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `maxTags` | `max-tags` | number |
| `allowDuplicates` | `allow-duplicates` | boolean |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [tags, setTags] = useState(["react", "design"]);

<TagInput value={tags} onChange={setTags} />
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Tag Input · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

It keeps its own list, so it works with nothing wired up. Pass `value` and `onChange` to follow or control it.

```tsx
const [tags, setTags] = useState(["react", "design"]);

<TagInput value={tags} onChange={setTags} />
```

#### Limits

`maxTags` caps the list (typing stops once it is reached); `allowDuplicates` lets the same tag in twice.

```tsx
<TagInput maxTags={3} />
<TagInput allowDuplicates />
```

#### Color and states

`color` tints the tags (same palette as Button), `invalid` draws the red error border and `disabled` locks it.

```tsx
<TagInput color="rose" />
<TagInput invalid />
<TagInput disabled />
```

### Tag Input · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Tag Input · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-invalid`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.

### Tag Input · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Tag Input · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `TagInput`: `root`, `tag`, `input`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

## Number Input

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `NumberInput`.

### Number Input · 1. Component Overview

A number field with − and + buttons, arrow-key stepping and min / max / precision handling.

- React: `import { NumberInput } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-number-input>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Number Input · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `NumberInput`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `number` | — | The current number (omit for empty). Also settable from outside; the input keeps its own value otherwise. |
| `min` | `number` | — | Smallest allowed value (default: no minimum). |
| `max` | `number` | — | Largest allowed value (default: no maximum). |
| `step` | `number` | `1` | How much the + / − buttons and the arrow keys change the value (default: 1). |
| `precision` | `number` | — | Decimal places to round to and show, e.g. 2 for prices (default: whatever you type). |
| `placeholder` | `string` | — | Placeholder shown while empty. |
| `size` | `NumberInputSize` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red border for error states (default: false). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the field and its buttons (default: false). |
| `onChange` | `(value: number \| undefined) => void` | — | Called with the new number (or undefined when the field is cleared) whenever it changes. |
| `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; input?: string; button?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NumberInput` | `onChange` | `(value: number \| undefined) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new number (or undefined when the field is cleared) whenever it changes. |
| `NumberInput` | `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `NumberInput` | `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `NumberInput` | `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `NumberInput` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Number Input · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Number Input · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NumberInput` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | Control height and text size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |

### Number Input · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `NumberInput` | `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red border for error states (default: false). |
| `NumberInput` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the field and its buttons (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable) · invalid (rose border/ring, `aria-invalid`).

### Number Input · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Number Input · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`NumberInput`** → `<l-number-input>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | number |
| `min` | `min` | number |
| `max` | `max` | number |
| `step` | `step` | number |
| `precision` | `precision` | number |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `invalid` | `invalid` | boolean |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [qty, setQty] = useState<number | undefined>(2);

<NumberInput value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={0} max={10} />
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Number Input · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Click − / +, press the up and down arrow keys, or type. The value is clamped to `min` and `max` when you leave the field.

```tsx
const [qty, setQty] = useState<number | undefined>(2);

<NumberInput value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={0} max={10} />
```

#### Step and precision

`step` sets how much a click changes the value; `precision` rounds to that many decimals — handy for prices.

```tsx
<NumberInput value={5} step={5} min={0} />
<NumberInput value={9.99} step={0.01} precision={2} min={0} />
```

#### Sizes and states

Three sizes, plus `invalid` and `disabled` states.

```tsx
<NumberInput size="sm" />
<NumberInput size="lg" />
<NumberInput invalid />
<NumberInput disabled />
```

### Number Input · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Number Input · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `spinbutton`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-invalid`, `aria-label`, `aria-valuemax`, `aria-valuemin`, `aria-valuenow`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.

### Number Input · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Number Input · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `NumberInput`: `root`, `input`, `button`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

## OTP Input

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `OtpInput`.

### OTP Input · 1. Component Overview

One box per character for verification codes. Typing moves forward, Backspace moves back, and pasting a code fills every box.

- React: `import { OtpInput } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-otp-input>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### OTP Input · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `OtpInput`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `length` | `number` | `6` | How many boxes (default: 6). |
| `value` | `string` | — | The code typed so far. Also settable from outside; the input keeps its own value otherwise. |
| `type` | `OtpInputType` | `"numeric"` | What may be typed: "numeric" (digits only, default) or "alphanumeric" (letters and digits). |
| `mask` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows dots instead of the characters, like a password (default: false). |
| `autoFocus` | `boolean` | `false` | Focuses the first box on mount (default: false). |
| `size` | `OtpInputSize` | `"md"` | Box size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red borders for error states (default: false). |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables every box (default: false). |
| `onChange` | `(value: string) => void` | — | Called with the code so far whenever a character is typed, pasted or deleted. |
| `onComplete` | `(value: string) => void` | — | Called once with the full code when every box is filled. |
| `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | — | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; box?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

| `OtpInput` | `onFocus` | `(e: FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `focus` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field gains focus — the native focus event (the web component's `focus` event, detail = the value). |
| `OtpInput` | `onInput` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `input` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called as the user edits the field — the native input event (the web component's `input` event, detail = the value). |
| `OtpInput` | `onInvalid` | `(e: FormEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` | `invalid` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called when the field fails validation — the native invalid event (the web component's `invalid` event, detail = the message). |
| `OtpInput` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `OtpInput` | `invalid` | `boolean` | `false` | Red borders for error states (default: false). |
| `OtpInput` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables every box (default: false). |

### OTP Input · 8. Slots / Children / Content

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<OtpInput onComplete={(code) => verify(code)} />
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### OTP Input · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

`onChange` reports the code so far; `onComplete` fires once with the full code when the last box is filled.

```tsx
<OtpInput onComplete={(code) => verify(code)} />
```

#### Length and type

```tsx
<OtpInput length={4} />
<OtpInput length={8} type="alphanumeric" size="sm" />
```

#### Mask and states

`mask` shows dots instead of the characters, `invalid` draws red borders (e.g. after a wrong code) and `disabled` locks it.

```tsx
<OtpInput mask />
<OtpInput invalid />
<OtpInput disabled />
```

### OTP Input · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-invalid`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.

### OTP Input · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### OTP Input · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `OtpInput`: `root`, `box`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

## Rating

*Category: Forms & Inputs.* Exports: `Rating`.

### Rating · 1. Component Overview

A row of stars for showing or giving a rating. Hover to preview, click to set (click the same star again to clear), arrow keys to adjust.

- React: `import { Rating } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-rating>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Rating · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Rating`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `number` | — | The current rating, 0 to `max`. Also settable from outside; the component keeps its own value otherwise. |
| `max` | `number` | `5` | Number of stars (default: 5). |
| `allowHalf` | `boolean` | `false` | Lets a rating be given in half steps, e.g. 3.5 (default: false). |
| `readOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows the rating without letting it be changed (default: false). |
| `size` | `RatingSize` | `"md"` | Star size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `label` | `string` | `"Rating"` | Accessible name for the group (default: "Rating"). |
| `onChange` | `(value: number) => void` | — | Called with the new rating when a star is chosen. Choosing the current rating again clears it to 0. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; star?: string }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

### Rating · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Rating` | `onChange` | `(value: number) => void` | `change` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new rating when a star is chosen. Choosing the current rating again clears it to 0. |
| `Rating` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Rating · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Rating · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Rating · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Rating` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | Star size: "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |

### Rating · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Rating` | `readOnly` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows the rating without letting it be changed (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Rating · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Rating · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Rating`** → `<l-rating>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `value` | number |
| `max` | `max` | number |
| `allowHalf` | `allow-half` | boolean |
| `readOnly` | `read-only` | boolean |

| `label` | `label` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState(3);

<Rating value={value} onChange={setValue} />
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Rating · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

It keeps its own value, so it works with nothing wired up. `onChange` reports the new rating.

```tsx
const [value, setValue] = useState(3);

<Rating value={value} onChange={setValue} />
```

#### Half stars and max

`allowHalf` lets a rating be given in half steps (the left half of a star is a half); `max` sets the number of stars.

```tsx
<Rating value={3.5} allowHalf />
<Rating value={7} max={10} size="sm" />
```

#### Read-only and sizes

`readOnly` just shows a rating, e.g. the average on a product. Three sizes.

```tsx
<Rating value={4.5} allowHalf readOnly />
<Rating value={4} readOnly size="lg" />
```

### Rating · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Rating · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-label`, `aria-valuemax`, `aria-valuemin`, `aria-valuenow`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.
- Labelling props: `label`.

### Rating · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Rating · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Rating`: `root`, `star`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: no per-component motion props.

### Color Picker · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Click the swatch for the system picker, a preset circle, or type a hex code (3 or 6 digits). `onChange` reports a normalized #rrggbb value.

```tsx
const [color, setColor] = useState("#6366f1");

<ColorPicker value={color} onChange={setColor} />
```

#### Presets and input

`presets` replaces the swatches (an empty list hides them); `showInput={false}` removes the hex field.

```tsx
<ColorPicker presets={["#0ea5e9", "#22c55e", "#f59e0b", "#ef4444"]} />
<ColorPicker showInput={false} />
```

- Focus: visible focus ring (`focus-visible`).

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `ColorPicker`: `root`, `swatch`, `input`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).

## Modal / Dialog

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `Modal`.

### Modal / Dialog · 1. Component Overview

The Modal / Dialog component of the library.

- React: `import { Modal } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-modal>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Modal / Dialog · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Modal`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` **required** | `boolean` | — | Whether the modal is shown (controlled) — renders nothing when false. |
| `onClose` **required** | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the user presses Escape, clicks the overlay, or clicks the close button; the consumer should set `open` to false. |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Content of the header title, rendered next to the close button. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Body content, rendered in the scrollable area below the header. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the dialog panel (same target as `classNames.root`). |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; overlay?: string; header?: string; title?: string; closeButton?: string; body?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Modal / Dialog · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Modal` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the user presses Escape, clicks the overlay, or clicks the close button; the consumer should set `open` to false. |
| `Modal` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Modal / Dialog · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Modal / Dialog · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Modal / Dialog · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Modal / Dialog · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Modal` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Whether the modal is shown (controlled) — renders nothing when false. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Modal / Dialog · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Modal` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Content of the header title, rendered next to the close button. |
| `Modal` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Body content, rendered in the scrollable area below the header. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Modal` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Modal / Dialog · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Modal`** → `<l-modal>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `heading` | `heading` | string |
| `className` | `class-name` | string |
| `classNames` | `class-names` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

### Modal / Dialog · 10. Complete Usage Examples

No packaged examples. Minimal use:

```tsx
import { Modal } from "lojee-ui";

<Modal open={…} onClose={…} children={…} />
```
### Modal / Dialog · 11. Component Composition

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Modal / Dialog · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: inherits the library's reduced-motion handling for transitions.

### Modal / Dialog · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Modal / Dialog · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Modal`: `root`, `overlay`, `header`, `title`, `closeButton`, `body`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Drawer

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `Drawer`.

### Drawer · 1. Component Overview

An edge-anchored panel that slides in over the page from any side.

- React: `import { Drawer } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-drawer>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Drawer · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Drawer`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` **required** | `boolean` | — | Whether the drawer is visible (controlled). |
| `onClose` **required** | `() => void` | — | Fires when the user requests closing (overlay click or close button) — the consumer should set `open` to false. |
| `position` | `DrawerPosition` | `"right"` | Edge the drawer slides in from: "left" \| "right" (default) \| "top" \| "bottom". |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Header title content; shown next to the close button. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Drawer body content. |
| `size` | `string` | — | Panel width (left/right) or height (top/bottom) as a CSS size, e.g. "320px". |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; overlay?: string; panel?: string; header?: string; title?: string; closeButton?: string; body?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Drawer · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Fires when the user requests closing (overlay click or close button) — the consumer should set `open` to false. |
| `Drawer` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Drawer · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `position` | `left`, `right`, `top`, `bottom` | `"right"` | Edge the drawer slides in from: "left" \| "right" (default) \| "top" \| "bottom". |

### Drawer · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Drawer · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `size` | `string` | — | Panel width (left/right) or height (top/bottom) as a CSS size, e.g. "320px". |

### Drawer · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Whether the drawer is visible (controlled). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Drawer · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Header title content; shown next to the close button. |
| `Drawer` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Drawer body content. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Drawer` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Drawer · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Drawer`** → `<l-drawer>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `heading` | `heading` | string |
| `position` | `position` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Position*):

**React**
```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Drawer open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} position="${position}" title="${position} drawer">
  <p>This drawer slid in from the ${position} edge.</p>
</Drawer>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Button label="Open ${position} drawer" id="open-drawer-btn"></l-Button>
<l-Drawer id="edge-drawer" position="${position}" heading="${position} drawer">
  <p>This drawer slid in from the ${position} edge.</p>
</l-Drawer>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const drawer = document.getElementById("edge-drawer");
  document.getElementById("open-drawer-btn")
    .addEventListener("click", () => { drawer.open = true; });
  drawer.addEventListener("close", () => { drawer.open = false; });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Drawer · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Position

Pick an edge for the panel to slide in from.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Drawer open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} position="${position}" title="${position} drawer">
  <p>This drawer slid in from the ${position} edge.</p>
</Drawer>
```

#### Custom size

Override the panel width via the size prop.

```tsx
<Drawer open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} position="right" size="480px" title="Wide drawer">
  <p>This drawer is 480px wide instead of the 320px default.</p>
</Drawer>
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — pick one, then close the overlay to see it play in reverse.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Open drawer" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<Drawer open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="Transition" position="right" ${trReact}>
  <p>A transition replaces the drawer's default slide.</p>
</Drawer>
```

### Drawer · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Drawer · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `dialog`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`, `aria-modal`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Drawer · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Drawer · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Drawer`: `root`, `overlay`, `panel`, `header`, `title`, `closeButton`, `body`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

### Sheet · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

A basic bottom sheet with a title and short body.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Sheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="Sheet title">
  <p>This is a basic bottom sheet.</p>
</Sheet>
```

#### Scrollable content

Long content scrolls inside the body while the header and handle stay put.

```tsx
<Sheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="Terms & conditions">
  <div className="space-y-4">
    {items.map((item) => <p key={item.id}>{item.text}</p>)}
  </div>
</Sheet>
```

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — pick one, then close the overlay to see it play in reverse.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Open sheet" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<Sheet open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} title="Transition" ${trReact}>
  <p>A transition replaces the sheet's default slide-up.</p>
</Sheet>
```
