## Popover

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `Popover`.

### Popover · 1. Component Overview

Click-triggered floating content, anchored to a trigger element — closes on outside click or Escape.

- React: `import { Popover } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-popover>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Popover · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Popover`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Content rendered inside the popover panel while it is open. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The trigger element; clicking it toggles the popover. |
| `position` | `PopoverPosition` | `"bottom"` | Which side of the trigger the panel appears on: "top", "bottom" (default), "left" or "right". |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — a click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; panel?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Popover · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Popover` | `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | `openchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — a click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `Popover` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Popover · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Popover` | `position` | `top`, `bottom`, `left`, `right` | `"bottom"` | Which side of the trigger the panel appears on: "top", "bottom" (default), "left" or "right". |

### Popover · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Popover · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Popover · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Popover` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Popover · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Popover` | `content` | `ReactNode` | Content rendered inside the popover panel while it is open. |
| `Popover` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The trigger element; clicking it toggles the popover. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Popover · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Popover`** → `<l-popover>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `content` | `content` | string |
| `position` | `position` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Positions*):

**React**
```tsx
<Popover content="Popover on top" position="top">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Top" />
</Popover>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Popover content="Popover on top" position="top">
  <l-Button variant="outline" label="Top"></l-Button>
</l-Popover>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Popover · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Positions

Click a button to see it.

```tsx
<Popover content="Popover on top" position="top">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Top" />
</Popover>
```

#### Rich content

The content prop accepts any ReactNode — including a small form.

```tsx
<Popover
  position="right"
  content={
    <div className="w-56">
      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-fg">Invite a teammate</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-fg-subtle">They'll get an email invite to join this workspace.</p>
      <input type="email" placeholder="name@company.com" />
      <Button className="mt-3 w-full" size="sm" label="Send invite" />
    </div>
  }
>
  <Button icon="plus" label="Invite" />
</Popover>
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — open and close each one to see it play both ways.

```tsx
<Popover transition="fade" content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Fade" />
</Popover>

<Popover transition="slide-up" content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Slide up" />
</Popover>

<Popover transition="zoom" transitionDelay={100} content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Zoom" />
</Popover>

<Popover transition="flip" content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Flip" />
</Popover>

<Popover transition="blur" content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Blur" />
</Popover>

<Popover transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} content="Hello from the popover">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Drop" />
</Popover>
```

### Popover · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Popover · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `dialog`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: none.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Popover · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Popover · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Popover`: `root`, `panel`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Dropdown Menu

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `DropdownMenu`, `DropdownMenuItem`.

### Dropdown Menu · 1. Component Overview

A trigger-anchored menu of actions — closes on selection, outside click, or Escape.

- React: `import { DropdownMenu } from "lojee-ui"` · `import { DropdownMenuItem } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-dropdown-menu>` · `<l-dropdown-menu-item>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Dropdown Menu · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `DropdownMenu`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `trigger` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Element that toggles the menu when clicked. |
| `align` | `DropdownMenuAlign` | `"start"` | Which edge of the trigger the menu aligns to: "start" (default) \| "end". |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | Menu content, typically DropdownMenuItem elements; closes on item click, outside click, or Escape. |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — a click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; menu?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

#### `DropdownMenuItem`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | — | Icon name, e.g. "pencil" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The item's label content. |
| `onClick` | `() => void` | — | Fires when the item is clicked (never for a disabled item); the parent menu closes afterwards. |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the item so it can't be clicked (default: false). |
| `danger` | `boolean` | `false` | Styles the item for a destructive action (rose text). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

### Dropdown Menu · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DropdownMenu` | `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | `openchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — a click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `DropdownMenu` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |
| `DropdownMenuItem` | `onClick` | `() => void` | — (React only) | Fires when the item is clicked (never for a disabled item); the parent menu closes afterwards. |

### Dropdown Menu · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DropdownMenu` | `align` | `start`, `end` | `"start"` | Which edge of the trigger the menu aligns to: "start" (default) \| "end". |

### Dropdown Menu · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Dropdown Menu · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Dropdown Menu · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DropdownMenu` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still toggle it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `DropdownMenuItem` | `disabled` | `boolean` | `false` | Disables the item so it can't be clicked (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed · disabled (dimmed, not focusable).

### Dropdown Menu · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `DropdownMenu` | `trigger` | `ReactNode` | Element that toggles the menu when clicked. |
| `DropdownMenu` | `children` | `ReactNode` | Menu content, typically DropdownMenuItem elements; closes on item click, outside click, or Escape. |
| `DropdownMenuItem` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The item's label content. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Dropdown Menu · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`DropdownMenu`** → `<l-dropdown-menu>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `align` | `align` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

**`DropdownMenuItem`** → `<l-dropdown-menu-item>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `icon` | string |
| `disabled` | `disabled` | boolean |
| `danger` | `danger` | boolean |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic menu*):

**React**
```tsx
<DropdownMenu trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="more-horizontal" iconOnly label="Actions" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil" onClick={() => edit()}>Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy" onClick={() => duplicate()}>Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="trash-2" danger onClick={() => remove()}>Delete</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-DropdownMenu id="actions-menu">
  <l-Button slot="trigger" variant="outline" icon="more-horizontal" iconOnly label="Actions"></l-Button>
  <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil" id="edit-item">Edit</l-DropdownMenuItem>
  <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="copy" id="duplicate-item">Duplicate</l-DropdownMenuItem>
  <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="trash-2" danger id="delete-item">Delete</l-DropdownMenuItem>
</l-DropdownMenu>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  document.getElementById("edit-item").addEventListener("click", () => edit());
  document.getElementById("duplicate-item").addEventListener("click", () => duplicate());
  document.getElementById("delete-item").addEventListener("click", () => remove());
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Dropdown Menu · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic menu

DropdownMenuItem children — Delete is a danger item.

```tsx
<DropdownMenu trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="more-horizontal" iconOnly label="Actions" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil" onClick={() => edit()}>Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy" onClick={() => duplicate()}>Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="trash-2" danger onClick={() => remove()}>Delete</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>
```

#### Align end

Aligns the panel to the trailing edge of the trigger.

```tsx
<DropdownMenu align="end" trigger={<Button icon="chevron-down" label="Options" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="settings" onClick={() => openSettings()}>Settings</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="share-2" onClick={() => share()}>Share</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem disabled>Archived</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — open and close each one to see it play both ways.

```tsx
<DropdownMenu transition="fade" trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Fade" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>

<DropdownMenu transition="slide-up" trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Slide up" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>

<DropdownMenu transition="zoom" transitionDelay={100} trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Zoom" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>

<DropdownMenu transition="flip" trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Flip" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>

<DropdownMenu transition="blur" trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Blur" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>

<DropdownMenu transition="drop" transitionDuration={700} trigger={<Button variant="outline" icon="chevron-down" label="Drop" />}>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil">Edit</DropdownMenuItem>
  <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy">Duplicate</DropdownMenuItem>
</DropdownMenu>
```

### Dropdown Menu · 11. Component Composition

- Built from `DropdownMenu`, `DropdownMenuItem` — use them together as shown in the examples above.
- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Dropdown Menu · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `menu`, `menuitem`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Dropdown Menu · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Dropdown Menu · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `DropdownMenu`: `root`, `menu` · `DropdownMenuItem`: `root`, `icon`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Context Menu

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `ContextMenu`.

### Context Menu · 1. Component Overview

Right-click anywhere on the target area to open a menu at the cursor — closes on selection, outside click, or Escape.

- React: `import { ContextMenu } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-context-menu>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Context Menu · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `ContextMenu`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The area that opens the menu when right-clicked (the trigger region). |
| `menu` | `ReactNode` | — | Menu content (typically DropdownMenuItem elements) shown at the pointer position on right-click; closes on item click, outside click, or Escape. |
| `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still open / close it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | — | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — right-click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; menu?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Context Menu · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ContextMenu` | `onOpenChange` | `(open: boolean) => void` | `openchange` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with the new open state whenever it opens or closes — right-click, outside click, Escape (the web component's `openchange` / `update` event, detail = true \| false). |
| `ContextMenu` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Context Menu · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Context Menu · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Context Menu · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Context Menu · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ContextMenu` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Opens or closes it from outside — set it whenever you want; the user can still open / close it, so it works with nothing wired up. Pair with `onOpenChange` for two-way binding. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Context Menu · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ContextMenu` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The area that opens the menu when right-clicked (the trigger region). |
| `ContextMenu` | `menu` | `ReactNode` | Menu content (typically DropdownMenuItem elements) shown at the pointer position on right-click; closes on item click, outside click, or Escape. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Context Menu · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`ContextMenu`** → `<l-context-menu>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic usage*):

**React**
```tsx
<ContextMenu
  menu={
    <>
      <DropdownMenuItem icon="copy" onClick={() => copy()}>Copy</DropdownMenuItem>
      <DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil" onClick={() => rename()}>Rename</DropdownMenuItem>
      <DropdownMenuItem icon="trash-2" danger onClick={() => remove()}>Delete</DropdownMenuItem>
    </>
  }
>
  <div>Right-click here</div>
</ContextMenu>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-ContextMenu>
  <div slot="menu">
    <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="copy" id="copy-item">Copy</l-DropdownMenuItem>
    <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="pencil" id="rename-item">Rename</l-DropdownMenuItem>
    <l-DropdownMenuItem icon="trash-2" danger id="delete-item">Delete</l-DropdownMenuItem>
  </div>
  <div>Right-click here</div>
</l-ContextMenu>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  document.getElementById("copy-item").addEventListener("click", () => copy());
  document.getElementById("rename-item").addEventListener("click", () => rename());
  document.getElementById("delete-item").addEventListener("click", () => remove());
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

## Command Menu

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `CommandMenu`.

### Command Menu · 1. Component Overview

A searchable, keyboard-navigable command palette overlay — the classic "Cmd+K" pattern.

- React: `import { CommandMenu } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-command-menu>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Command Menu · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `CommandMenu`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` **required** | `boolean` | — | Whether the command palette is visible (controlled); nothing is rendered when false, and the search resets each time it opens. |
| `onClose` **required** | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the menu should close: on Escape, on backdrop click, or after an item is selected. |
| `items` **required** | `CommandMenuItem[]` | — | The commands to list; typing filters them by label, description and keywords, and Enter runs the highlighted one. |
| `placeholder` | `string` | `"Type a command or search…"` | Placeholder text of the search input (default: "Type a command or search…"). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root overlay element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; overlay?: string; panel?: string; input?: string; icon?: string; list?: string; item?: string; activeItem?: string; empty?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Command Menu · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CommandMenu` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the menu should close: on Escape, on backdrop click, or after an item is selected. |
| `CommandMenu` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Command Menu · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Command Menu · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Command Menu · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Command Menu · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CommandMenu` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Whether the command palette is visible (controlled); nothing is rendered when false, and the search resets each time it opens. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Command Menu · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Command Menu · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`CommandMenu`** → `<l-command-menu>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `items` | `items` | json (JSON string or object property) |
| `placeholder` | `placeholder` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic usage*):

**React**
```tsx
const items: CommandMenuItem[] = [
  { label: "New file", icon: "file", shortcut: "⌘N", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Create project", icon: "plus", shortcut: "⌘P", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Search everywhere", icon: "search", shortcut: "⌘K", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Invite team members", icon: "users", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Share this page", icon: "share-2", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Open settings", icon: "settings", shortcut: "⌘,", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Star this repo", icon: "star", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Delete workspace", icon: "trash-2", disabled: true, onSelect: () => {} },
];

<CommandMenu open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} items={items} />
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<button id="open-command-menu-btn">Search commands…</button>
<l-CommandMenu id="cmd-menu"></l-CommandMenu>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const menu = document.getElementById("cmd-menu");
  menu.items = [
    { label: "New file", icon: "file", shortcut: "⌘N", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Create project", icon: "plus", shortcut: "⌘P", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Search everywhere", icon: "search", shortcut: "⌘K", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Invite team members", icon: "users", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Share this page", icon: "share-2", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Open settings", icon: "settings", shortcut: "⌘,", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Star this repo", icon: "star", onSelect: () => {} },
    { label: "Delete workspace", icon: "trash-2", disabled: true, onSelect: () => {} },
  ];

  document.getElementById("open-command-menu-btn")
    .addEventListener("click", () => { menu.open = true; });
  menu.addEventListener("close", () => { menu.open = false; });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Command Menu · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic usage

Click the trigger, or note that in a real app this would typically be bound to a global Cmd+K shortcut.

```tsx
const items: CommandMenuItem[] = [
  { label: "New file", icon: "file", shortcut: "⌘N", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Create project", icon: "plus", shortcut: "⌘P", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Search everywhere", icon: "search", shortcut: "⌘K", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Invite team members", icon: "users", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Share this page", icon: "share-2", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Open settings", icon: "settings", shortcut: "⌘,", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Star this repo", icon: "star", onSelect: () => {} },
  { label: "Delete workspace", icon: "trash-2", disabled: true, onSelect: () => {} },
];

<CommandMenu open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} items={items} />
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — pick one, then close the overlay to see it play in reverse.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
const items: CommandMenuItem[] = [
  { label: "New file", icon: "file", shortcut: "⌘N" },
  { label: "Create project", icon: "plus", shortcut: "⌘P" },
  { label: "Open settings", icon: "settings", shortcut: "⌘," }
];

<Button label="Open command menu" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<CommandMenu open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} items={items} ${trReact} />
```

### Command Menu · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.
- Data-driven: pass `items` instead of child components (works the same as a Web Component).

### Command Menu · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `dialog`, `listbox`, `option`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-disabled`, `aria-modal`, `aria-selected`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Command Menu · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Command Menu · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `CommandMenu`: `root`, `overlay`, `panel`, `input`, `icon`, `list`, `item`, `activeItem`, `empty`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## Alert Dialog

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `AlertDialog`.

### Alert Dialog · 1. Component Overview

A focused confirmation dialog with an icon, title, description, and Cancel/Confirm actions.

- React: `import { AlertDialog } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-alert-dialog>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Alert Dialog · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `AlertDialog`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` **required** | `boolean` | — | Whether the dialog is visible (controlled); nothing is rendered when false. |
| `onClose` **required** | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the dialog should close: on Cancel, on Confirm (after `onConfirm`), on overlay click, or on Escape. |
| `title` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Dialog heading. |
| `description` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional supporting text shown beneath the title. |
| `variant` | `AlertDialogVariant` | `"default"` | "default" or "destructive" — destructive uses a warning icon and a destructive confirm button (default: "default"). |
| `confirmLabel` | `string` | `"Confirm"` | Text of the confirm button (default: "Confirm"). |
| `cancelLabel` | `string` | `"Cancel"` | Text of the cancel button (default: "Cancel"). |
| `onConfirm` | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the confirm button is clicked, just before `onClose` fires. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the dialog panel. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; overlay?: string; icon?: string; title?: string; description?: string; footer?: string; confirmButton?: string; cancelButton?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides (`root`, `overlay`, `icon`, `title`, `description`, `footer`, `confirmButton`, `cancelButton`) — merged after the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Alert Dialog · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AlertDialog` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the dialog should close: on Cancel, on Confirm (after `onConfirm`), on overlay click, or on Escape. |
| `AlertDialog` | `onConfirm` | `() => void` | `confirm` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the confirm button is clicked, just before `onClose` fires. |
| `AlertDialog` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Alert Dialog · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AlertDialog` | `variant` | `default`, `destructive` | `"default"` | "default" or "destructive" — destructive uses a warning icon and a destructive confirm button (default: "default"). |

### Alert Dialog · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Alert Dialog · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Alert Dialog · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AlertDialog` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Whether the dialog is visible (controlled); nothing is rendered when false. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Alert Dialog · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AlertDialog` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Dialog heading. |
| `AlertDialog` | `description` | `ReactNode` | Optional supporting text shown beneath the title. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AlertDialog` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Alert Dialog · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`AlertDialog`** → `<l-alert-dialog>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `heading` | `heading` | string |
| `description` | `description` | string |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `confirmLabel` | `confirm-label` | string |
| `cancelLabel` | `cancel-label` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Default*):

**React**
```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Open confirmation" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<AlertDialog
  open={open}
  onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
  title="Save changes?"
  description="Your changes will be applied immediately."
  onConfirm={() => { /* persist */ }}
/>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Button label="Open confirmation" id="open-confirm-btn"></l-Button>
<l-AlertDialog
  id="save-dialog"
  heading="Save changes?"
  description="Your changes will be applied immediately."></l-AlertDialog>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const dialog = document.getElementById("save-dialog");
  document.getElementById("open-confirm-btn")
    .addEventListener("click", () => { dialog.open = true; });
  dialog.addEventListener("close", () => { dialog.open = false; });
  dialog.addEventListener("confirm", () => {
    /* persist */
    dialog.open = false;
  });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Alert Dialog · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Default

A neutral confirmation prompt.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Open confirmation" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<AlertDialog
  open={open}
  onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
  title="Save changes?"
  description="Your changes will be applied immediately."
  onConfirm={() => { /* persist */ }}
/>
```

#### Destructive

Destructive variant with a red confirm button and warning icon.

```tsx
<AlertDialog
  open={open}
  onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
  variant="destructive"
  title="Delete item?"
  description="This will permanently remove the item. This action cannot be undone."
  confirmLabel="Delete"
  onConfirm={() => { /* delete */ }}
/>
```

#### Custom labels

Custom confirm/cancel labels.

```tsx
<AlertDialog
  open={open}
  onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
  title="Leave without saving?"
  description="You have unsaved changes that will be lost."
  confirmLabel="Leave"
  cancelLabel="Stay"
  onConfirm={() => { /* navigate away */ }}
/>
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — pick one, then close the overlay to see it play in reverse.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Open alert dialog" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<AlertDialog
  open={open}
  onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
  title="Discard changes?"
  description="Your edits will be lost."
  ${trReact}
/>
```

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Alert Dialog · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `alertdialog`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-modal`.
- Keyboard: has keyboard handling (e.g. Escape / arrow keys) — see the docs page for the exact keys.
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Alert Dialog · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Alert Dialog · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `AlertDialog`: `root`, `overlay`, `icon`, `title`, `description`, `footer`, `confirmButton`, `cancelButton`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Tooltip

*Category: Overlays.* Exports: `Tooltip`.

### Tooltip · 1. Component Overview

Hover-triggered floating text, positioned via pure CSS — no JS state, no positioning library.

- React: `import { Tooltip } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-tooltip>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Tooltip · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Tooltip`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | What the tooltip bubble displays. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The trigger element the tooltip is attached to; hovering it shows the bubble. |
| `position` | `TooltipPosition` | `"top"` | Which side of the trigger the bubble appears on: "top", "bottom", "left" or "right" (default: "top"). |
| `size` | `"xs" \| "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" \| "xl"` | `"md"` | Bubble size: "xs" (tiny, 10px text), "sm" (compact, 11px), "md" (default, 12px), "lg" (roomier, 14px) or "xl" (large, 16px). |
| `delayMs` | `number` | `150` | Show delay in ms, snapped to the nearest Tailwind `delay-*` utility. |
| `color` | `ColorName \| "neutral"` | `"accent"` | Bubble background/text color — same palette as Button (default: "accent", which follows the theme's accent color), or "neutral" for the theme-inverted bubble (dark in light mode, light in dark mode). |
| `portal` | `boolean` | `false` | Render the bubble through a portal (a fixed-position layer outside the trigger's ancestors), so it is never clipped by a scrolling or `overflow: hidden` parent — e.g. inside a collapsed sidebar (default: false). |
| `open` | `boolean` | `false` | Keep the bubble showing ("active") whether or not the trigger is hovered or focused — for a hint that should be visible right away. Not supported together with `portal` (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; bubble?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Tooltip · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Tooltip · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Tooltip` | `position` | `top`, `bottom`, `left`, `right` | `"top"` | Which side of the trigger the bubble appears on: "top", "bottom", "left" or "right" (default: "top"). |

### Tooltip · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Tooltip` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Bubble background/text color — same palette as Button (default: "accent", which follows the theme's accent color), or "neutral" for the theme-inverted bubble (dark in light mode, light in dark mode). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Tooltip · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Tooltip` | `size` | `xs`, `sm`, `md`, `lg`, `xl` | `"md"` | Bubble size: "xs" (tiny, 10px text), "sm" (compact, 11px), "md" (default, 12px), "lg" (roomier, 14px) or "xl" (large, 16px). |

### Tooltip · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Tooltip` | `open` | `boolean` | `false` | Keep the bubble showing ("active") whether or not the trigger is hovered or focused — for a hint that should be visible right away. Not supported together with `portal` (default: false). |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Tooltip · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Tooltip` | `content` | `ReactNode` | What the tooltip bubble displays. |
| `Tooltip` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The trigger element the tooltip is attached to; hovering it shows the bubble. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Tooltip · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Tooltip`** → `<l-tooltip>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `content` | `content` | string |
| `position` | `position` | string |
| `size` | `size` | string |
| `delayMs` | `delay-ms` | number |
| `color` | `color` | string |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Positions*):

**React**
```tsx
<Tooltip content="Tooltip on top" position="top">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Top" />
</Tooltip>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Tooltip content="Tooltip on top" position="top">
  <l-Button variant="outline" label="Top"></l-Button>
</l-Tooltip>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Tooltip · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Positions

Hover a button to see it.

```tsx
<Tooltip content="Tooltip on top" position="top">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Top" />
</Tooltip>
```

#### Sizes

```tsx
<Tooltip content="Extra small" size="xs">
  <Button variant="outline" label="xs" />
</Tooltip>
<Tooltip content="Small" size="sm">
  <Button variant="outline" label="sm" />
</Tooltip>
<Tooltip content="Medium">
  <Button variant="outline" label="md (default)" />
</Tooltip>
<Tooltip content="Large" size="lg">
  <Button variant="outline" label="lg" />
</Tooltip>
<Tooltip content="Extra large" size="xl">
  <Button variant="outline" label="xl" />
</Tooltip>
```

#### Colors

```tsx
<Tooltip content="Indigo tooltip" color="indigo">
  <Button variant="outline" label="Indigo" />
</Tooltip>
```

#### Delay

Snapped to the nearest Tailwind delay-* step.

```tsx
<Tooltip content="Waits a bit" delayMs={500}>...</Tooltip>
```

#### Any trigger

Any element can be the trigger, not just Button.

```tsx
<Tooltip content="This works on plain text too">
  <span>Hover this text</span>
</Tooltip>
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

### Tooltip · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Tooltip · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `tooltip`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Tooltip · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `lg`, `md`, `sm`, `xl`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Tooltip · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Tooltip`: `root`, `bubble`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with `color`; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`.

## ── Feedback ──

## Alert

*Category: Feedback.* Exports: `Alert`.

### Alert · 1. Component Overview

An inline, non-dismissing-by-default banner message for surfacing status, feedback, or warnings inline in a page.

- React: `import { Alert } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-alert>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Alert · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Alert`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `pulseColor` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |
| `pulseGradientTo` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | — | Second color — turns the pulse ring and spinning border into a gradient from `pulseColor` to this (default: solid `pulseColor`). |
| `variant` | `AlertVariant` | `"info"` | Visual/semantic tone: "info" \| "success" \| "warning" \| "error" \| "accent" — "accent" follows the theme accent color (default: "info"). |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional bold heading shown above the description. |
| `children` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | The description/body. |
| `icon` | `string \| false` | — | Icon name to override the variant's default icon, or `false` to hide the icon entirely. |
| `closable` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a dismiss (X) button (default: false); the alert does not hide itself, so remove it in `onClose`. |
| `onClose` | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the dismiss button is clicked. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class names applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; title?: string; description?: string; closeButton?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Alert · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the dismiss button is clicked. |

### Alert · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `variant` | `info`, `success`, `warning`, `error`, `accent` | `"info"` | Visual/semantic tone: "info" \| "success" \| "warning" \| "error" \| "accent" — "accent" follows the theme accent color (default: "info"). |

### Alert · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `pulseColor` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | — | Color of the animation (pulse ring, glow, spinning border): a `ColorName` or any CSS color (default: the component's own color). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Alert · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Alert · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `animated` | `AnimatedVariant` | — | Attention animation: "glow" \| "pulse" \| "sweep" \| "bounce" \| "float" \| "wiggle" \| "border-spin" (default: none). Respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. |
| `Alert` | `closable` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a dismiss (X) button (default: false); the alert does not hide itself, so remove it in `onClose`. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Alert · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Optional bold heading shown above the description. |
| `Alert` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The description/body. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Alert` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Alert · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Alert`** → `<l-alert>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `animated` | `animated` | string |
| `pulseColor` | `pulse-color` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Variants*):

**React**
```tsx
<Alert variant="info" title="Heads up">This is an informational message.</Alert>
<Alert variant="success" title="Saved">Your changes have been saved.</Alert>
<Alert variant="warning" title="Careful">This action may have unintended side effects.</Alert>
<Alert variant="error" title="Something went wrong">We couldn't process your request. Please try again.</Alert>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Alert variant="info" title="Heads up">This is an informational message.</l-Alert>
<l-Alert variant="success" title="Saved">Your changes have been saved.</l-Alert>
<l-Alert variant="warning" title="Careful">This action may have unintended side effects.</l-Alert>
<l-Alert variant="error" title="Something went wrong">We couldn't process your request. Please try again.</l-Alert>

<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Alert · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Variants

Each variant carries its own color treatment and default icon.

```tsx
<Alert variant="info" title="Heads up">This is an informational message.</Alert>
<Alert variant="success" title="Saved">Your changes have been saved.</Alert>
<Alert variant="warning" title="Careful">This action may have unintended side effects.</Alert>
<Alert variant="error" title="Something went wrong">We couldn't process your request. Please try again.</Alert>
```

#### Without a title

Title is optional — omit it for a compact, single-line alert.

```tsx
<Alert variant="info">A new version is available. Refresh to update.</Alert>
```

#### Closable

Set closable and handle onClose to let the user dismiss it.

```tsx
const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true);

{visible && (
  <Alert variant="warning" title="Unsaved changes" closable onClose={() => setVisible(false)}>
    You have unsaved changes that will be lost if you navigate away.
  </Alert>
)}
```

#### Custom icon

Override the default per-variant icon, or hide it entirely.

```tsx
<Alert variant="info" title="New feature" icon="bell">
  We just shipped keyboard shortcuts. Press "?" to see them.
</Alert>
<Alert variant="info" title="No icon" icon={false}>
  This alert renders without a leading icon.
</Alert>
```

#### Custom styling

Per-part class overrides via classNames.

```tsx
<Alert
  variant="info"
  title="Styled alert"
  classNames={{
    root: "border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-900 dark:border-indigo-900 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 dark:text-indigo-200",
    icon: "text-indigo-500",
  }}
>
  This alert's border, background, and icon pick up custom colors via classNames.
</Alert>
```

_2 more examples are on the docs page._

- Standalone: drop it into any layout (Card, Section, Grid, Container, App shell) or inside forms and overlays.

### Alert · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `alert`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Alert · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Alert · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Alert`: `root`, `icon`, `title`, `description`, `closeButton`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Toast

*Category: Feedback.* Exports: `Toast`.

### Toast · 1. Component Overview

A transient, fixed-position, auto-dismissing notification. Controlled visibility like Modal/Drawer — render as many as you need for a stack of your own.

- React: `import { Toast } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-toast>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Toast · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Toast`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` **required** | `boolean` | — | Whether the toast is visible; this is fully controlled, so the parent owns the state. |
| `onClose` **required** | `() => void` | — | Called with no arguments when the toast's close button is pressed or its `duration` elapses — set `open` to false here. |
| `variant` | `ToastVariant` | `"info"` | Visual/semantic tone (default: "info"). |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | — | Bold heading line shown above the body. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The description/body. |
| `duration` | `number` | `4000` | Auto-dismiss after this many ms; 0 or omitted disables auto-dismiss. Default: 4000. |
| `position` | `ToastPosition` | `"bottom-right"` | Screen anchor (default: "bottom-right"). |
| `icon` | `string \| false` | — | Icon name to override the variant's default icon, or `false` to hide the icon entirely. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) appended to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; title?: string; description?: string; closeButton?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Toast · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Toast` | `onClose` | `() => void` | `close` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Called with no arguments when the toast's close button is pressed or its `duration` elapses — set `open` to false here. |
| `Toast` | `onUpdate` | — | `update` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | The value was committed (the native `change`); `detail` = the new value (`true` / `false` for a checkbox or switch, the value `string` / `number` otherwise). React: `onChange`. |

### Toast · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Toast` | `variant` | `info`, `success`, `warning`, `error` | `"info"` | Visual/semantic tone (default: "info"). |
| `Toast` | `position` | `ToastPosition` | `"bottom-right"` | Screen anchor (default: "bottom-right"). |

### Toast · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Toast · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Toast · 7. Component States

| Component | Prop | Type | Default | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Toast` | `open` | `boolean` | — | Whether the toast is visible; this is fully controlled, so the parent owns the state. |

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Toast · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Toast` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Bold heading line shown above the body. |
| `Toast` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The description/body. |

Web Component extras (slots and plain HTML attributes accepted by the `<l-*>` tag in addition to the props above):

| Component | Name | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Toast` | `heading` | `string` | Text shown as the title. Named `heading` here because `title` is a native HTMLElement attribute. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Toast · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Toast`** → `<l-toast>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `open` | boolean |
| `variant` | `variant` | string |
| `heading` | `heading` | string |
| `duration` | `duration` | number |
| `position` | `position` | string |
| `icon` | `icon` | string |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |
| `transitionDuration` | `transition-duration` | number |
| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Variants*):

**React**
```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Success" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<Toast open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} variant="success" title="Success">
  This is a success toast notification.
</Toast>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Button label="Success" id="open-toast-btn"></l-Button>
<l-Toast id="success-toast" variant="success" title="Success">
  This is a success toast notification.
</l-Toast>

<script type="module">
  import "lojee-ui/elements";

  const toast = document.getElementById("success-toast");
  document.getElementById("open-toast-btn")
    .addEventListener("click", () => { toast.open = true; });
  toast.addEventListener("close", () => { toast.open = false; });
</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Toast · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Variants

Each variant carries its own color treatment and default icon. Auto-dismisses after 4s.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Success" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<Toast open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} variant="success" title="Success">
  This is a success toast notification.
</Toast>
```

#### Position

Anchor the toast to any corner or edge of the screen via position.

```tsx
<Toast open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} position="top-center" title="Positioned toast">
  Anchored to top-center.
</Toast>
```

#### No auto-dismiss

Set duration={0} to disable auto-dismiss and rely on the manual close button only.

```tsx
<Toast open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} variant="warning" title="Action required" duration={0}>
  This toast stays open until you dismiss it.
</Toast>
```

#### Transitions

Enter/exit transitions via `transition` (with `transitionDuration` / `transitionDelay`) — pick one, then close the overlay to see it play in reverse.

```tsx
const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

<Button label="Show toast" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} />
<Toast open={open} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} variant="success" title="Saved" ${trReact}>
  Your changes have been saved.
</Toast>
```

### Toast · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Toast · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: `status`.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-label`, `aria-live`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Toast · 13. Responsive Behavior

- Breakpoint modifiers in its source: `sm`.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Toast · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Toast`: `root`, `icon`, `title`, `description`, `closeButton`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Notification

*Category: Feedback.* Exports: `Notification`.

### Notification · 1. Component Overview

A static, richer notification-feed list item, meant to be rendered inside a list, dropdown, or panel you build.

- React: `import { Notification } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-notification>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Notification · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Notification`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `string` | `"bell"` | Icon name, e.g. "bell" — see src/core/icons.ts for the available set (default: "bell"). |
| `title` **required** | `ReactNode` | — | Bold heading line of the notification. |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | — | The description/body. |
| `timestamp` | `string` | — | Freeform display string, e.g. "2m ago" — not a real Date, just text. |
| `unread` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows a small colored dot near the title. |
| `onDismiss` | `() => void` | — | Shows a close (x) button when provided. |
| `actions` | `ReactNode` | — | Optional row of action buttons/links below the description. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; icon?: string; title?: string; description?: string; timestamp?: string; dismissButton?: string; actions?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect` — see §1.7.

### Notification · 3. Events

| Component | React callback | Signature | Web Component event | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Notification` | `onDismiss` | `() => void` | `dismiss` (CustomEvent, payload in `detail`) | Shows a close (x) button when provided. |

### Notification · 4. Variants

_This component has no variant prop — it has a single look (restyle it with colors / `classNames`)._

### Notification · 5. Colors and Theming

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set. It has no color prop of its own.

### Notification · 6. Sizes

_No size prop — it sizes to its container / content (see `className` to constrain it)._

### Notification · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Notification · 8. Slots / Children / Content

| Component | React prop | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Notification` | `title` | `ReactNode` | Bold heading line of the notification. |
| `Notification` | `children` | `ReactNode` | The description/body. |
| `Notification` | `actions` | `ReactNode` | Optional row of action buttons/links below the description. |

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Notification · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Notification`** → `<l-notification>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `icon` | `icon` | string |
| `timestamp` | `timestamp` | string |
| `unread` | `unread` | boolean |
| `transition` | `transition` | string |

| `transitionDelay` | `transition-delay` | number |
| `hoverEffect` | `hover-effect` | string |

Minimal use in each target (from the first docs example, *Basic*):

**React**
```tsx
<Notification title="New comment" icon="mail">
  Alex left a comment on your document.
</Notification>
```
**Plain HTML / JavaScript**
```html
<l-Notification title="New comment" icon="mail">
  Alex left a comment on your document.
</l-Notification>

<script type="module">import "lojee-ui/elements";</script>
```
Vue and Angular use the same `<l-*>` tag and attributes — see the framework templates in §1.3.

### Notification · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Basic

Icon, title, and description.

```tsx
<Notification title="New comment" icon="mail">
  Alex left a comment on your document.
</Notification>
```

#### Unread

A small accent-colored dot next to the title marks it unread.

```tsx
<Notification title="New follower" unread>
  Jordan started following you.
</Notification>
```

#### With timestamp

A freeform display string, not a real Date.

```tsx
<Notification title="Deploy finished" timestamp="2m ago">
  Your latest deploy to production finished successfully.
</Notification>
```

#### With actions

Pass a row of buttons or links via actions.

```tsx
<Notification
  title="Team invite"
  timestamp="10m ago"
  unread
  actions={
    <>
      <Button size="sm" label="Accept" />
      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" label="Decline" />
    </>
  }
>
  Priya invited you to join the "Design" team.
</Notification>
```

#### Dismissible

Pass onDismiss to show a close (x) button.

```tsx
const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true);

{visible && (
  <Notification title="Storage almost full" onDismiss={() => setVisible(false)}>
    You're using 92% of your available storage.
  </Notification>
)}
```

_1 more examples are on the docs page._

### Notification · 11. Component Composition

- Combined in its own examples with: `Button`.

### Notification · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA roles used: none beyond native element semantics.
- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-label`.
- Keyboard: native keyboard behavior of the underlying element (Tab to focus, Enter / Space to activate).
- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.
- Motion: respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.

### Notification · 13. Responsive Behavior

- No breakpoint-specific rules — it is fluid and adapts to its container width.
- Mobile-first; touch targets keep a comfortable minimum size.

### Notification · 14. Customization

- `className` adds classes to the root; `classNames` overrides individual parts — `Notification`: `root`, `icon`, `title`, `description`, `timestamp`, `dismissButton`, `actions`. Conflicting Tailwind utilities passed here win (tailwind-merge).
- Recolor with the theme accent; restyle globally with the theme tokens or `data-design="clay"`.
- Motion: `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay`, `hoverEffect`.

## Thinking

*Category: Feedback.* Exports: `Thinking`.

### Thinking · 1. Component Overview

An AI "thinking" indicator — animated dots, wave bars, an orb or a shimmering label, with optional rotating status lines and a timer.

- React: `import { Thinking } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-thinking>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Thinking · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `Thinking`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `label` | `string` | `"Thinking"` | Text next to the indicator (default: "Thinking"). Ignored while `steps` is set. |
| `variant` | `ThinkingVariant` | `"dots"` | Indicator style: "dots" (bouncing dots), "wave" (audio-style bars), "orb" (pulsing gradient orb) or "shimmer" (the label itself glows in a sweep) (default: "dots"). |
| `size` | `ThinkingSize` | `"md"` | "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |
| `color` | `ColorName` | `"accent"` | Color of the indicator, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `steps` | `string[]` | — | Status lines to cycle through, e.g. ["Reading the file", "Planning", "Writing the answer"] — replaces `label` and loops every `stepInterval` ms. |
| `stepInterval` | `number` | `2200` | How long each step stays on screen, in ms (default: 2200). |
| `showElapsed` | `boolean` | `false` | Appends a running timer — "Thinking · 4s" (default: false). |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra class name(s) applied to the root element. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; indicator?: string; label?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

### Thinking · 3. Events

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Thinking · 4. Variants

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Thinking` | `variant` | `dots`, `wave`, `orb`, `shimmer` | `"dots"` | Indicator style: "dots" (bouncing dots), "wave" (audio-style bars), "orb" (pulsing gradient orb) or "shimmer" (the label itself glows in a sweep) (default: "dots"). |

### Thinking · 5. Colors and Theming

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Thinking` | `color` | `slate`, `gray`, `indigo`, `violet`, `blue`, `cyan`, `emerald`, `teal`, `amber`, `orange`, `rose`, `pink`, `accent` | `"accent"` | Color of the indicator, one of the built-in ColorNames (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |

Follows the global theme (§1.4): it recolors with the accent, switches with light / dark mode, and takes the Claymorphism look when `data-design="clay"` is set.

### Thinking · 6. Sizes

| Component | Prop | Values | Default | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Thinking` | `size` | `sm`, `md`, `lg` | `"md"` | "sm" \| "md" \| "lg" (default: "md"). |

### Thinking · 7. Component States

_No dedicated state props — the visual states are default, hover, focus-visible and active._

Standard visual states: default · hover · focus-visible (ring) · active/pressed.

### Thinking · 8. Slots / Children / Content

_No children or slots — it is configured entirely through props._

In a Web Component, the element's light-DOM text/children go into the default slot; a prop that accepts a node (e.g. `header`, `footer`) can usually also be filled with a child carrying `slot="<name>"`.

### Thinking · 9. Framework Interfaces

**`Thinking`** → `<l-thinking>`

| React prop | HTML attribute | Attribute type |
| --- | --- | --- |

No packaged examples. Minimal use:

```tsx
<Thinking />
```

### Thinking · 12. Accessibility

- ARIA attributes set by the component: `aria-hidden`, `aria-live`.

- Focus: uses the browser focus outline / the underlying control's ring.

## Progress Bar

*Category: Feedback.* Exports: `ProgressBar`.

### Progress Bar · 1. Component Overview

A simple, non-slotted progress indicator for showing determinate or indeterminate progress.

- React: `import { ProgressBar } from "lojee-ui"`
- Web Component: `<l-progress-bar>` (after `import "lojee-ui/elements"`)

### Progress Bar · 2. Props and Interfaces

#### `ProgressBar`

| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `number` | — | 0–100 (or 0–max). Omit and set `indeterminate` for an unknown-duration loading bar. |
| `max` | `number` | `100` | default 100 |
| `size` | `ProgressBarSize` | `"md"` | Controls track height (default: "md"). |
| `color` | `ColorName \| (string & {})` | `"accent"` | A built-in ColorName or any CSS color such as "#8b5cf6" (default: "accent" — follows the theme accent). |
| `showLabel` | `boolean` | `false` | Shows the percentage as text. |
| `striped` | `boolean` | `false` | Diagonal-stripe texture on the filled bar. |
| `indeterminate` | `boolean` | `false` | Animated sweeping bar, ignores `value`. |
| `className` | `string` | — | Extra CSS class(es) added to the root element, merged before `classNames.root`. |
| `classNames` | `{ root?: string; track?: string; bar?: string; label?: string; }` | — | Per-part class overrides — merged after (and win over) the built-in styling. |

Also accepts the shared motion props `transition`, `transitionDuration`, `transitionDelay` — see §1.7.

_No component-specific events._ Native DOM events (`click`, `input`, `focus`…) bubble from the rendered element as usual.

### Progress Bar · 10. Complete Usage Examples

#### Sizes

size controls the track height.

```tsx
<ProgressBar value={40} size="sm" />
<ProgressBar value={60} size="md" />
<ProgressBar value={80} size="lg" />
```

#### Colors

Every bar follows the theme accent — change the accent from the top bar and they all update.

```tsx
<ProgressBar value={70} />
<ProgressBar value={55} />
<ProgressBar value={30} />
```

#### Striped

A diagonal-stripe texture on the filled bar.

```tsx
<ProgressBar value={65} striped />
```

#### Indeterminate

An animated sweeping bar for unknown-duration loading.

```tsx
<ProgressBar indeterminate />
```

#### With label

Shows the current percentage as text.

```tsx
<ProgressBar value={45} showLabel />
```
